2208:     def decision_walk_forward(self, window=120, methods=None, progress_callback=None, cancel_event=None):
2209:         """
2210:         Simulação histórica walk-forward sem look-ahead.
2211: 
2212:         Para cada extração-base selecionada, executa exatamente os métodos atuais
2213:         usando somente informação anterior àquela base. O resultado imediatamente
2214:         operacional seguinte é usado apenas DEPOIS para auditoria.
2215: 
2216:         window é a quantidade máxima de transições operacionais mais recentes.
2217:         Nenhuma configuração é otimizada dentro deste método.
2218:         """

2220:             window=max(1,min(500,int(window)))
2221:         except Exception:
2222:             window=120

2225:             requested=[m for m in method_order if m in set(methods)]

2227:         else:
2228:             methods=method_order

2208:     def decision_walk_forward(self, window=120, methods=None, progress_callback=None, cancel_event=None):
2209:         """
2210:         Simulação histórica walk-forward sem look-ahead.
2211: 
2212:         Para cada extração-base selecionada, executa exatamente os métodos atuais
2213:         usando somente informação anterior àquela base. O resultado imediatamente
2214:         operacional seguinte é usado apenas DEPOIS para auditoria.
2215:

2217:         Nenhuma configuração é otimizada dentro deste método.
2218:         """

2220:             window=max(1,min(500,int(window)))

1730:     def _decision_metrics_for_signal_list(values):
1731:         n=len(values)
1732:         if not n:
1733:             return {"rounds":0,"avg_coverage":0.0,"pct_2plus":0.0,"pct_3plus":0.0}
1734:         return {
1735:             "rounds":n,
1736:             "avg_coverage":sum(values)/n,
1737:             "pct_2plus":sum(v>=2 for v in values)/n*100.0,
1738:             "pct_3plus":sum(v>=3 for v in values)/n*100.0,
1739:         }
1740: 
1741:     def decision_champion_challenger(self, window=60, min_rounds=12):
1742:         """Compara desafiante e Reset nas MESMAS rodadas. Nunca promove automaticamente."""
1743:         rows=self._decision_audited_rows(window=window)
1744:         champion="Reset Cobertura"
1745:         candidates=("Puxada Combinada","Similaridade")
1746:         comparisons=[]
1747:         for challenger in candidates:
1748:             champ_hits=[]
1749:             chal_hits=[]
1750:             for row in rows:
1751:                 try:
1752:                     signals=json.loads(row.get("signals_json") or "{}")
1753:                 except Exception:
1754:                     continue
1755:                 cs=signals.get(champion) or {}
1756:                 ds=signals.get(challenger) or {}
1757:                 if not cs.get("available") or not ds.get("available"):
1758:                     continue
1759:                 if cs.get("coverage_hits") is None or ds.get("coverage_hits") is None:
1760:                     continue
1761:                 champ_hits.append(int(cs.get("coverage_hits") or 0))
1762:                 chal_hits.append(int(ds.get("coverage_hits") or 0))
1763:             cm=self._decision_metrics_for_signal_list(champ_hits)
1764:             dm=self._decision_metrics_for_signal_list(chal_hits)

===== METHOD Database._decision_clip [1591:1592] =====
1591:     def _decision_clip(value, low=0.0, high=100.0):
1592:         return max(float(low), min(float(high), float(value)))

===== METHOD Database._decision_signal_payload [1595:1622] =====
1595:     def _decision_signal_payload(result, kind):
1596:         if not result:
1597:             return {"available": False, "groups": [], "animals": [], "kind": kind}
1598:         selected = result.get("selected") or []
1599:         groups = [int(r["grupo"]) for r in selected if r.get("grupo")]
1600:         payload = {
1601:             "available": bool(groups),
1602:             "groups": groups,
1603:             "animals": [BICHOS.get(g, str(g)) for g in groups],
1604:             "kind": kind,
1605:         }
1606:         if kind == "reset":
1607:             payload["scores"] = [round(float(r.get("score_pct") or 0), 6) for r in selected]
1608:             payload["training_transitions"] = int(result.get("training_transitions") or 0)
1609:             payload["pair3_matches"] = int(result.get("pair3_matches") or 0)
1610:             payload["context_mode"] = result.get("context_mode")
1611:         elif kind == "pull":
1612:             payload["source_counts"] = [int(r.get("source_count") or 0) for r in selected]
1613:             payload["sum_prob"] = [round(float(r.get("sum_prob") or 0), 6) for r in selected]
1614:         elif kind == "similarity":
1615:             payload["weighted_shares"] = [round(float(r.get("weighted_share") or 0), 6) for r in selected]
1616:             payload["candidate_count"] = int(result.get("candidate_count") or 0)
1617:             payload["top_days"] = [
1618:                 {"date": d.get("date"), "score": round(float(d.get("score") or 0), 6)}
1619:                 for d in (result.get("top_days") or [])[:5]
1620:             ]
1621:             payload["positional"] = True
1622:         return payload

1630:             return "—"

===== METHOD Database._decision_audited_rows [1632:1652] =====
1632:     def _decision_audited_rows(self, window=None, target_hour=None, weekday=None):
1633:         """Retorna snapshots auditados, aplicando filtros antes da janela."""
1634:         with self.connect() as con:
1635:             rows = con.execute(
1636:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE status='AUDITADO' "
1637:                 "ORDER BY target_data DESC, target_hora DESC, id DESC"
1638:             ).fetchall()
1639:         out = []
1640:         for row in rows:
1641:             d = dict(row)
1642:             if target_hour and str(d.get("target_hora") or "") != str(target_hour):
1643:                 continue
1644:             if weekday and self._decision_weekday_label(d.get("target_data")) != str(weekday):
1645:                 continue
1646:             out.append(d)
1647:         if window not in (None, "all", "Todos"):
1648:             try:
1649:                 out = out[:max(1, int(window))]
1650:             except Exception:
1651:                 pass
1652:         return out

===== METHOD Database._decision_performance_from_rows [1655:1690] =====
1655:     def _decision_performance_from_rows(rows):
1656:         accum = {}
1657:         for row in rows:
1658:             try:
1659:                 signals = json.loads(row.get("signals_json") or "{}")
1660:             except Exception:
1661:                 signals = {}
1662:             for name, sig in signals.items():
1663:                 if not sig.get("available"):
1664:                     continue
1665:                 a = accum.setdefault(name, {
1666:                     "method": name, "rounds": 0, "coverage_hits": 0,
1667:                     "rounds_2plus": 0, "rounds_3plus": 0,
1668:                     "position_hits": 0, "position_rounds": 0,
1669:                 })
1670:                 hits = int(sig.get("coverage_hits") or 0)
1671:                 a["rounds"] += 1
1672:                 a["coverage_hits"] += hits
1673:                 a["rounds_2plus"] += int(hits >= 2)
1674:                 a["rounds_3plus"] += int(hits >= 3)
1675:                 if sig.get("position_hits") is not None:
1676:                     a["position_hits"] += int(sig.get("position_hits") or 0)
1677:                     a["position_rounds"] += 1
1678:         out = []
1679:         for a in accum.values():
1680:             rounds = max(1, a["rounds"])
1681:             a["avg_coverage"] = a["coverage_hits"] / rounds
1682:             a["pct_2plus"] = a["rounds_2plus"] / rounds * 100.0
1683:             a["pct_3plus"] = a["rounds_3plus"] / rounds * 100.0
1684:             a["avg_position"] = (
1685:                 a["position_hits"] / a["position_rounds"]
1686:                 if a["position_rounds"] else None
1687:             )
1688:             out.append(a)
1689:         out.sort(key=lambda r: (-r["avg_coverage"], -r["pct_2plus"], r["method"]))
1690:         return out

===== METHOD Database.decision_champion_challenger [1741:1790] =====
1741:     def decision_champion_challenger(self, window=60, min_rounds=12):
1742:         """Compara desafiante e Reset nas MESMAS rodadas. Nunca promove automaticamente."""
1743:         rows=self._decision_audited_rows(window=window)
1744:         champion="Reset Cobertura"
1745:         candidates=("Puxada Combinada","Similaridade")
1746:         comparisons=[]
1747:         for challenger in candidates:
1748:             champ_hits=[]
1749:             chal_hits=[]
1750:             for row in rows:
1751:                 try:
1752:                     signals=json.loads(row.get("signals_json") or "{}")
1753:                 except Exception:
1754:                     continue
1755:                 cs=signals.get(champion) or {}
1756:                 ds=signals.get(challenger) or {}
1757:                 if not cs.get("available") or not ds.get("available"):
1758:                     continue
1759:                 if cs.get("coverage_hits") is None or ds.get("coverage_hits") is None:
1760:                     continue
1761:                 champ_hits.append(int(cs.get("coverage_hits") or 0))
1762:                 chal_hits.append(int(ds.get("coverage_hits") or 0))
1763:             cm=self._decision_metrics_for_signal_list(champ_hits)
1764:             dm=self._decision_metrics_for_signal_list(chal_hits)
1765:             comparisons.append({
1766:                 "challenger":challenger, "paired_rounds":len(champ_hits),
1767:                 "champion":cm, "challenger_metrics":dm,
1768:                 "avg_diff":dm["avg_coverage"]-cm["avg_coverage"],
1769:                 "p2_diff":dm["pct_2plus"]-cm["pct_2plus"],
1770:                 "p3_diff":dm["pct_3plus"]-cm["pct_3plus"],
1771:             })
1772:         comparisons.sort(key=lambda x:(x["avg_diff"],x["p2_diff"],x["p3_diff"],x["paired_rounds"]), reverse=True)
1773:         best=comparisons[0] if comparisons else None
1774:         if not best or best["paired_rounds"] < int(min_rounds):
1775:             status="AMOSTRA INSUFICIENTE"
1776:             recommendation=f"Manter {champion} oficial; acumular pelo menos {int(min_rounds)} confrontos pareados."
1777:         elif best["avg_diff"] >= 0.20 and best["p2_diff"] >= 0 and best["p3_diff"] >= -5:
1778:             status="CANDIDATO FORTE"
1779:             recommendation="Desafiante mostrou vantagem consistente. Manter em sombra e exigir confirmação antes de qualquer promoção manual."
1780:         elif best["avg_diff"] >= 0.05 or best["p2_diff"] >= 5:
1781:             status="DESAFIANTE EM OBSERVAÇÃO"
1782:             recommendation="Há sinal de vantagem, mas ainda não é suficiente para trocar o método oficial."
1783:         else:
1784:             status="CAMPEÃO ESTÁVEL"
1785:             recommendation=f"{champion} segue sem evidência suficiente para ser substituído."
1786:         return {
1787:             "window":window, "official_champion":champion, "best":best,
1788:             "comparisons":comparisons, "status":status, "recommendation":recommendation,
1789:             "min_rounds":int(min_rounds),
1790:         }

===== METHOD Database.decision_change_detection [1792:1831] =====
1792:     def decision_change_detection(self, recent_window=12, baseline_window=30):
1793:         """Detector simples de mudança: compara bloco recente com bloco anterior."""
1794:         rows=self._decision_audited_rows(window="Todos")
1795:         methods=("Reset Cobertura","Puxada Combinada","Similaridade")
1796:         out=[]
1797:         for method in methods:
1798:             values=[]
1799:             for row in rows:
1800:                 try:
1801:                     sig=(json.loads(row.get("signals_json") or "{}").get(method) or {})
1802:                 except Exception:
1803:                     continue
1804:                 if sig.get("available") and sig.get("coverage_hits") is not None:
1805:                     values.append(int(sig.get("coverage_hits") or 0))
1806:             recent=values[:int(recent_window)]
1807:             baseline=values[int(recent_window):int(recent_window)+int(baseline_window)]
1808:             rm=self._decision_metrics_for_signal_list(recent)
1809:             bm=self._decision_metrics_for_signal_list(baseline)
1810:             avg_delta=rm["avg_coverage"]-bm["avg_coverage"] if baseline else 0.0
1811:             p2_delta=rm["pct_2plus"]-bm["pct_2plus"] if baseline else 0.0
1812:             if len(recent)<6 or len(baseline)<12:
1813:                 status="AMOSTRA INSUFICIENTE"
1814:             elif avg_delta <= -0.50 or (avg_delta <= -0.30 and p2_delta <= -15):
1815:                 status="QUEDA FORTE"
1816:             elif avg_delta <= -0.25 or p2_delta <= -12:
1817:                 status="ATENÇÃO"
1818:             elif avg_delta >= 0.25 and p2_delta >= 10:
1819:                 status="MELHORA"
1820:             else:
1821:                 status="ESTÁVEL"
1822:             out.append({
1823:                 "method":method, "status":status,
1824:                 "recent_rounds":len(recent), "baseline_rounds":len(baseline),
1825:                 "recent_avg":rm["avg_coverage"], "baseline_avg":bm["avg_coverage"],
1826:                 "avg_delta":avg_delta, "recent_p2":rm["pct_2plus"],
1827:                 "baseline_p2":bm["pct_2plus"], "p2_delta":p2_delta,
1828:             })
1829:         priority={"QUEDA FORTE":0,"ATENÇÃO":1,"AMOSTRA INSUFICIENTE":2,"ESTÁVEL":3,"MELHORA":4}
1830:         out.sort(key=lambda r:(priority.get(r["status"],9),r["method"]))
1831:         return {"recent_window":recent_window,"baseline_window":baseline_window,"rows":out}

===== METHOD Database._decision_groups_from_item [1834:1851] =====
1834:     def _decision_groups_from_item(game, item):
1835:         if item.get("grupo") not in (None, ""):
1836:             try:
1837:                 return [int(item["grupo"])]
1838:             except Exception:
1839:                 pass
1840:         kind=str(game.get("tipo") or "")
1841:         tokens=[int(x) for x in re.findall(r"\d+", str(item.get("numero") or ""))]
1842:         groups=[]
1843:         if "Grupo" in kind or kind.startswith("Passe"):
1844:             groups=[x for x in tokens if 1 <= x <= 25]
1845:         else:
1846:             for token in tokens:
1847:                 try:
1848:                     groups.append(int(grupo_da_dezena(token % 100)))
1849:                 except Exception:
1850:                     pass
1851:         return groups

===== METHOD Database.decision_concentration_from_generation [2098:2125] =====
2098:     def decision_concentration_from_generation(self, generation):
2099:         generation=generation or {}
2100:         rows=generation.get("rows") or []
2101:         game={"tipo":generation.get("kind") or "Centena"}
2102:         counter=Counter()
2103:         for row in rows:
2104:             for group in self._decision_groups_from_item(game,row):
2105:                 if 1 <= int(group) <= 25:
2106:                     counter[int(group)] += 1
2107:         total=sum(counter.values())
2108:         if not total:
2109:             return {"games":0,"items":len(rows),"group_mentions":0,"score":0.0,"label":"SEM LEITURA","top":[],"effective_groups":0.0,"top2_share":0.0}
2110:         shares={g:c/total for g,c in counter.items()}
2111:         hhi=sum(v*v for v in shares.values())
2112:         effective=1.0/hhi if hhi else 0.0
2113:         ordered=sorted(counter.items(), key=lambda kv:(-kv[1],kv[0]))
2114:         top=[{"grupo":g,"bicho":BICHOS.get(g,str(g)),"count":c,"share":shares[g]*100.0} for g,c in ordered[:5]]
2115:         top1=top[0]["share"] if top else 0.0
2116:         top2=sum(r["share"] for r in top[:2])
2117:         hhi_scaled=self._decision_clip((hhi-0.04)/(1.0-0.04)*100.0)
2118:         score=round(self._decision_clip(0.70*top2 + 0.30*hhi_scaled),1)
2119:         if top1 >= 45 or top2 >= 70 or score >= 65:
2120:             label="ALTA"
2121:         elif top1 >= 35 or top2 >= 55 or score >= 50:
2122:             label="MODERADA"
2123:         else:
2124:             label="BAIXA"
2125:         return {"games":1,"items":len(rows),"group_mentions":total,"score":score,"label":label,"top":top,"top1_share":top1,"top2_share":top2,"effective_groups":effective}

2161:         top2=sum(r["share"] for r in top[:2])
2162:         # score combina dependência dos dois líderes com a concentração global (HHI).

===== METHOD Database._walk_forward_metric_summary [2179:2206] =====
2179:     def _walk_forward_metric_summary(records):
2180:         """Resume registros de um método sem misturar rodadas indisponíveis."""
2181:         valid=[r for r in records if r.get("available")]
2182:         n=len(valid)
2183:         if not n:
2184:             return {
2185:                 "rounds":0,"avg_coverage":0.0,"pct_1plus":0.0,"pct_2plus":0.0,
2186:                 "pct_3plus":0.0,"pct_4plus":0.0,"pct_5":0.0,"avg_position":None,
2187:                 "avg_random_expected":0.0,"uplift_vs_random":0.0,"errors":len(records),
2188:             }
2189:         hits=[int(r.get("coverage_hits") or 0) for r in valid]
2190:         pos=[int(r.get("position_hits") or 0) for r in valid if r.get("position_hits") is not None]
2191:         random_expected=[float(r.get("random_expected") or 0.0) for r in valid]
2192:         avg=sum(hits)/n
2193:         baseline=(sum(random_expected)/len(random_expected)) if random_expected else 0.0
2194:         return {
2195:             "rounds":n,
2196:             "avg_coverage":avg,
2197:             "pct_1plus":sum(v>=1 for v in hits)/n*100.0,
2198:             "pct_2plus":sum(v>=2 for v in hits)/n*100.0,
2199:             "pct_3plus":sum(v>=3 for v in hits)/n*100.0,
2200:             "pct_4plus":sum(v>=4 for v in hits)/n*100.0,
2201:             "pct_5":sum(v>=5 for v in hits)/n*100.0,
2202:             "avg_position":(sum(pos)/len(pos)) if pos else None,
2203:             "avg_random_expected":baseline,
2204:             "uplift_vs_random":avg-baseline,
2205:             "errors":len(records)-n,
2206:         }

===== METHOD Database.decision_walk_forward [2208:2382] =====
2208:     def decision_walk_forward(self, window=120, methods=None, progress_callback=None, cancel_event=None):
2209:         """
2210:         Simulação histórica walk-forward sem look-ahead.
2211: 
2212:         Para cada extração-base selecionada, executa exatamente os métodos atuais
2213:         usando somente informação anterior àquela base. O resultado imediatamente
2214:         operacional seguinte é usado apenas DEPOIS para auditoria.
2215: 
2216:         window é a quantidade máxima de transições operacionais mais recentes.
2217:         Nenhuma configuração é otimizada dentro deste método.
2218:         """
2219:         try:
2220:             window=max(1,min(500,int(window)))
2221:         except Exception:
2222:             window=120
2223:         method_order=("Reset Cobertura","Puxada Combinada","Similaridade")
2224:         if methods:
2225:             requested=[m for m in method_order if m in set(methods)]
2226:             methods=tuple(requested or method_order)
2227:         else:
2228:             methods=method_order
2229: 
2230:         draws=self._draws_in_order()

2234:             if i < 35 or not self._is_operational_draw(base) or len(base.get("prizes") or []) < 5:
2235:                 continue
2236:             # Walk-forward rigoroso: se a rodada operacional que ERA esperada
2237:             # após a base não existe no histórico, pula o caso. Não usa uma
2238:             # rodada posterior só porque hoje sabemos que ela existe.
2239:             expected=self._reset_expected_target(base)
2240:             target_index=key_to_index.get((expected["data"],expected["sorteio"],expected["hora"]))

2244:             if len(target.get("prizes") or []) < 5:
2245:                 continue
2246:             candidates.append((i,base,target_index,target))

2249:         details=[]

2251: 
2252:         for done,(base_index,base,target_index,target) in enumerate(candidates, start=1):
2253:             if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
2254:                 cancelled=True
2255:                 break

2257:                 try:
2258:                     progress_callback(done-1,total,{

2262:                     pass

2272:             }

2275:                     cancelled=True

2280:                         positional=None
2281:                     elif method == "Puxada Combinada":

2287:                         positional=sum(1 for a,b in zip(predicted_slots,target_groups) if int(a)==int(b))
2288:                     predicted=[int(r["grupo"]) for r in (result.get("selected") or []) if r.get("grupo")]
2289:                     pred_unique=set(predicted)
2290:                     coverage=len(pred_unique & target_unique)
2291:                     random_expected=(len(pred_unique)*len(target_unique)/25.0) if pred_unique else 0.0
2292:                     row["methods"][method]={
2293:                         "available":True,"groups":predicted,
2294:                         "animals":[BICHOS.get(g,str(g)) for g in predicted],

2296:                         "random_expected":random_expected,
2297:                         "lookahead_safe":bool(result.get("lookahead_safe",True)),
2298:                     }
2299:                 except Exception as exc:

2301:                         "available":False,"groups":[],"animals":[],"coverage_hits":None,
2302:                         "position_hits":None,"random_expected":0.0,"error":str(exc),
2303:                     }

2305:             if progress_callback:
2306:                 try:
2307:                     progress_callback(done,total,{
2308:                         "data":base["data"],"sorteio":base["sorteio"],"hora":base["hora"],
2309:                     })
2310:                 except Exception:
2311:                     pass
2312:             if cancelled:
2313:                 break
2314: 
2315:         summary=[]
2316:         for method in methods:
2317:             records=[row["methods"].get(method,{"available":False}) for row in details]
2318:             met=self._walk_forward_metric_summary(records)
2319:             met["method"]=method
2320:             summary.append(met)
2321:         summary.sort(key=lambda r:(-r["avg_coverage"],-r["pct_2plus"],-r["pct_3plus"],r["method"]))

2330:             paired_summary.append({
2331:                 "method":method,
2332:                 "rounds":len(vals),
2333:                 "avg_coverage":(sum(vals)/len(vals)) if vals else 0.0,
2334:                 "pct_2plus":(sum(v>=2 for v in vals)/len(vals)*100.0) if vals else 0.0,

2336:             })

2339: 
2340:         # Robustez descritiva: compara a primeira e a segunda metade cronológica
2341:         # do mesmo recorte. Não é teste de significância e não altera o método.
2342:         split=max(1,len(details)//2) if details else 0
2343:         robustness=[]

2346:             second=[r["methods"].get(method,{"available":False}) for r in details[split:]]

2348:             sm=self._walk_forward_metric_summary(second)
2349:             robustness.append({
2350:                 "method":method,"first_rounds":fm["rounds"],"second_rounds":sm["rounds"],
2351:                 "first_avg":fm["avg_coverage"],"second_avg":sm["avg_coverage"],
2352:                 "delta":sm["avg_coverage"]-fm["avg_coverage"],
2353:                 "first_p2":fm["pct_2plus"],"second_p2":sm["pct_2plus"],
2354:             })
2355: 
2356:         date_from=details[0]["target"]["data"] if details else None
2357:         date_to=details[-1]["target"]["data"] if details else None
2358:         return {
2359:             "window":window,
2360:             "requested_rounds":window,
2361:             "eligible_rounds":total,
2362:             "simulated_rounds":len(details),
2363:             "cancelled":cancelled,
2364:             "methods":list(methods),
2365:             "summary":summary,

===== METHOD Database._decision_confidence [2384:2467] =====
2384:     def _decision_confidence(self, reset_result, pull_result, similarity_result):
2385:         reset_groups = [int(r["grupo"]) for r in (reset_result or {}).get("selected", [])]
2386:         pull_groups = [int(r["grupo"]) for r in (pull_result or {}).get("selected", [])]
2387:         sim_groups = [int(r["grupo"]) for r in (similarity_result or {}).get("selected", [])]
2388:         reset_set = set(reset_groups)
2389: 
2390:         overlaps = []
2391:         overlap_detail = {}
2392:         for name, groups in (("Puxada Combinada", pull_groups), ("Similaridade", sim_groups)):
2393:             if groups:
2394:                 overlap = len(reset_set & set(groups))
2395:                 denom = max(1, min(5, len(set(groups))))
2396:                 score = overlap / denom * 100.0
2397:                 overlaps.append(score)
2398:                 overlap_detail[name] = {"overlap": overlap, "score": score}
2399:         convergence = sum(overlaps) / len(overlaps) if overlaps else 35.0
2400: 
2401:         ranking = (reset_result or {}).get("ranking") or []
2402:         selected = (reset_result or {}).get("selected") or []
2403:         top_scores = [float(r.get("score_pct") or 0) for r in selected]
2404:         mean_top = sum(top_scores) / len(top_scores) if top_scores else 0.0
2405:         lift_component = self._decision_clip((mean_top - 18.0) / 18.0 * 100.0)
2406:         gap = 0.0
2407:         if len(ranking) >= 6:
2408:             gap = float(ranking[4].get("score_pct") or 0) - float(ranking[5].get("score_pct") or 0)
2409:         separation = self._decision_clip(gap / 4.0 * 100.0)
2410:         reset_strength = 0.75 * lift_component + 0.25 * separation
2411: 
2412:         transitions = int((reset_result or {}).get("training_transitions") or 0)
2413:         transition_score = self._decision_clip(transitions / 240.0 * 100.0)
2414:         context_mode = (reset_result or {}).get("context_mode")
2415:         if context_mode == "pair3":
2416:             pair3 = int((reset_result or {}).get("pair3_matches") or 0)
2417:             context_specific = self._decision_clip(pair3 / 20.0 * 100.0)
2418:         else:
2419:             pair3 = 0
2420:             context_specific = 65.0
2421:         context_support = 0.55 * transition_score + 0.45 * context_specific
2422: 
2423:         perf = self.decision_method_performance(window=30)
2424:         reset_perf = next((r for r in perf["rows"] if r["method"] == "Reset Cobertura"), None)
2425:         evidence_rounds = int(reset_perf["rounds"]) if reset_perf else 0
2426:         if reset_perf:
2427:             avg_hits = float(reset_perf["avg_coverage"])
2428:             evidence = self._decision_clip((avg_hits - 0.5) / 2.5 * 100.0)
2429:         else:
2430:             evidence = 50.0
2431: 
2432:         raw = (
2433:             0.35 * convergence
2434:             + 0.25 * reset_strength
2435:             + 0.20 * context_support
2436:             + 0.20 * evidence
2437:         )
2438:         # Sem amostra prospectiva suficiente a Central não pode declarar confiança alta.
2439:         cap = 68.0 if evidence_rounds < 3 else (74.0 if evidence_rounds < 10 else 100.0)
2440:         score = min(raw, cap)
2441:         score = round(self._decision_clip(score), 1)
2442:         if score >= 75:
2443:             label, recommendation = "ALTA", "Sinais fortes e consistentes"
2444:         elif score >= 60:
2445:             label, recommendation = "MODERADA", "Leitura normal; manter disciplina"
2446:         elif score >= 45:
2447:             label, recommendation = "BAIXA", "Cautela: sinais parcialmente divididos"
2448:         else:
2449:             label, recommendation = "MUITO BAIXA", "Sinais divididos; não elevar exposição"
2450:         return {
2451:             "score": score,
2452:             "label": label,
2453:             "recommendation": recommendation,
2454:             "components": {
2455:                 "convergence": round(convergence, 1),
2456:                 "reset_strength": round(reset_strength, 1),
2457:                 "context_support": round(context_support, 1),
2458:                 "prospective_evidence": round(evidence, 1),
2459:                 "prospective_rounds": evidence_rounds,
2460:                 "overlap_detail": overlap_detail,
2461:                 "mean_reset_score": round(mean_top, 3),
2462:                 "reset_gap_5_6": round(gap, 3),
2463:                 "training_transitions": transitions,
2464:                 "pair3_matches": pair3,
2465:                 "confidence_cap": cap,
2466:             },
2467:         }

===== METHOD Database.build_decision_reading [2469:2502] =====
2469:     def build_decision_reading(self, base_draw=None):
2470:         base = base_draw or self.latest_operational_draw()
2471:         if not base:
2472:             raise ValueError("Não há extração operacional suficiente para a Central de Decisão.")
2473:         reset = pull = sim = None
2474:         errors = {}
2475:         try:
2476:             reset = self.method_reset_coverage_v1(base["data"], base["sorteio"], base["hora"], top_n=5)
2477:         except Exception as exc:
2478:             errors["Reset Cobertura"] = str(exc)
2479:         try:
2480:             pull = self.method_convergencia_g5(base["data"], base["sorteio"], base["hora"], top_n=5)
2481:         except Exception as exc:
2482:             errors["Puxada Combinada"] = str(exc)
2483:         try:
2484:             sim = self.method_similarity_day(base["data"], base["sorteio"], base["hora"], top_days=12)
2485:         except Exception as exc:
2486:             errors["Similaridade"] = str(exc)
2487:         if not reset:
2488:             raise ValueError(errors.get("Reset Cobertura") or "Reset Cobertura indisponível.")
2489:         target = dict(reset.get("target") or self._reset_expected_target(base))
2490:         confidence = self._decision_confidence(reset, pull, sim)
2491:         signals = {
2492:             "Reset Cobertura": self._decision_signal_payload(reset, "reset"),
2493:             "Puxada Combinada": self._decision_signal_payload(pull, "pull"),
2494:             "Similaridade": self._decision_signal_payload(sim, "similarity"),
2495:         }
2496:         return {
2497:             "base": base,
2498:             "target": target,
2499:             "confidence": confidence,
2500:             "signals": signals,
2501:             "errors": errors,
2502:         }

===== METHOD Database._decision_row_to_dict [2551:2571] =====
2551:     def _decision_row_to_dict(self, row):
2552:         if row is None:
2553:             return None
2554:         d = dict(row)
2555:         json_defaults = {
2556:             "components_json": {},
2557:             "signals_json": {},
2558:             "contextual_json": {},
2559:             "contextual_audit_json": {},
2560:             "meta_json": {},
2561:             "meta_audit_json": {},
2562:             "result_groups_json": [],
2563:         }
2564:         for field, default in json_defaults.items():
2565:             raw = d.get(field)
2566:             key = field.replace("_json", "")
2567:             try:
2568:                 d[key] = json.loads(raw) if raw else copy.deepcopy(default)
2569:             except Exception:
2570:                 d[key] = copy.deepcopy(default)
2571:         return d

===== METHOD Database._meta_squash [2598:2603] =====
2598:     def _meta_squash(value, scale):
2599:         try:
2600:             value=max(0.0,float(value)); scale=max(1e-9,float(scale))
2601:         except Exception:
2602:             return 0.0
2603:         return value/(value+scale)

===== METHOD Database._meta_sigmoid [2606:2608] =====
2606:     def _meta_sigmoid(z):
2607:         z=max(-35.0,min(35.0,float(z)))
2608:         return 1.0/(1.0+exp(-z))

===== METHOD Database._meta_round_key [2611:2618] =====
2611:     def _meta_round_key(data, hora, fallback=0):
2612:         try:
2613:             day=datetime.strptime(str(data),"%Y-%m-%d").toordinal()
2614:         except Exception:
2615:             day=0
2616:         m=re.search(r"(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?",str(hora or ""))
2617:         minutes=(int(m.group(1))*60 + int(m.group(2) or 0)) if m else int(fallback or 0)
2618:         return day,minutes,int(fallback or 0)

2622:         try:

===== METHOD Database._meta_feature_vector [2627:2688] =====
2627:     def _meta_feature_vector(self, group, signals, historical, base_draw):

2629:         signals=signals or {}
2630:         features=[]

2636:         rscores=reset.get("scores") or []
2637:         rstrength=self._meta_squash(rscores[pos] if pos is not None and pos < len(rscores) else 0,50.0)

2639:

2645:         pprob=pull.get("sum_prob") or []

2647:         pstrength=self._meta_squash(pprob[pos] if pos is not None and pos < len(pprob) else 0,60.0)
2648:         features += [psel,prank,psources,pstrength]

2654:         shares=sim.get("weighted_shares") or []
2655:         sstrength=self._meta_squash(shares[pos] if pos is not None and pos < len(shares) else 0,30.0)
2656:         features += [ssel,srank,sstrength]

2663:                     hpos=i; hrow=row; break
2664:             except Exception:
2665:                 pass

2671:

2674:

2677:             try: base_groups.append(int(p.get("grupo")))
2678:             except Exception: pass
2679:         count=base_groups.count(group)

2681: 
2682:         table1_votes = sum(
2683:             1 for source_group in base_groups
2684:             if group in META_TABLE1_PULLS.get(int(source_group), ())
2685:         )
2686:         table1_strength = float(table1_votes) / float(max(1, len(base_groups)))
2687:         features += [table1_strength]
2688:         return [float(v) for v in features]

===== METHOD Database._meta_fit_logit [2691:2730] =====
2691:     def _meta_fit_logit(cls, examples, epochs=240, l2=0.025):
2692:         examples=list(examples or [])
2693:         if not examples:
2694:             return None
2695:         n_features=len(examples[0][0])
2696:         if any(len(x)!=n_features for x,_,_ in examples):
2697:             raise ValueError("Exemplos Meta com dimensões incompatíveis.")
2698:         pos=sum(1 for _,y,_ in examples if int(y)==1)
2699:         neg=len(examples)-pos
2700:         if not pos or not neg:
2701:             return None
2702:         positive_weight=min(4.0,max(1.0,neg/max(1,pos)))
2703:         w=[0.0]*n_features
2704:         bias=0.0
2705:         lr=0.18
2706:         for epoch in range(max(40,int(epochs))):
2707:             gb=0.0; gw=[0.0]*n_features; total=0.0
2708:             for x,y,sample_weight in examples:
2709:                 y=1.0 if int(y) else 0.0
2710:                 sw=max(0.05,float(sample_weight or 1.0))*(positive_weight if y else 1.0)
2711:                 z=bias+sum(a*b for a,b in zip(w,x))
2712:                 p=cls._meta_sigmoid(z)
2713:                 err=p-y
2714:                 gb += sw*err
2715:                 for j,val in enumerate(x): gw[j] += sw*err*val
2716:                 total += sw
2717:             total=max(total,1e-9)
2718:             bias -= lr*(gb/total)
2719:             for j in range(n_features):
2720:                 w[j] -= lr*((gw[j]/total) + float(l2)*w[j])
2721:             lr=max(0.025,lr*0.992)
2722:         return {
2723:             "schema":1,
2724:             "kind":"native_logit_l2",
2725:             "intercept":round(bias,9),
2726:             "coefficients":[round(v,9) for v in w],
2727:             "positive_weight":round(positive_weight,6),
2728:             "examples":len(examples),
2729:             "positive_examples":pos,
2730:         }

===== METHOD Database._meta_model_score [2733:2739] =====
2733:     def _meta_model_score(cls, model, features):
2734:         if not model:
2735:             return 0.0
2736:         z=float(model.get("intercept") or 0.0)
2737:         coefs=model.get("coefficients") or []
2738:         z += sum(float(a)*float(b) for a,b in zip(coefs,features))
2739:         return cls._meta_sigmoid(z)*100.0

===== METHOD Database._meta_training_records [2741:2777] =====
2741:     def _meta_training_records(self, before_target=None, limit=160):
2742:         """Monta exemplos somente de snapshots já auditados e anteriores ao alvo."""
2743:         with self.connect() as con:
2744:             rows=con.execute(

2746:                 "AND result_groups_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(result_groups_json)<>'' "
2747:                 "ORDER BY target_data ASC,id ASC"

2750:         if before_target:
2751:             cutoff=self._meta_round_key(before_target.get("data"),before_target.get("hora"),10**9)
2752:         parsed=[]
2753:         for row in rows:
2754:             d=self._decision_row_to_dict(row)
2755:             if cutoff and self._meta_round_key(d.get("target_data"),d.get("target_hora"),d.get("id") or 0) >= cutoff:
2756:                 continue

2759:                 continue

2761:             if not all((signals.get(name) or {}).get("available") for name in ("Reset Cobertura","Puxada Combinada","Similaridade")):
2762:                 continue
2763:             base=self.get_draw(d.get("base_data"),d.get("base_sorteio"),d.get("base_hora"))
2764:             if not base:
2765:                 continue
2766:             try:
2767:                 historical=self.method_historico_concentrado_v01(
2768:                     d.get("base_data"),d.get("base_sorteio"),d.get("base_hora"),top_n=5
2769:                 )
2770:             except Exception:
2771:                 historical={"selected":[]}
2772:             parsed.append({
2773:                 "target_data":d.get("target_data"),"target_hora":d.get("target_hora"),
2774:                 "signals":signals,"historical":historical,"base_draw":base,
2775:                 "result_groups":result_groups,
2776:             })
2777:         return parsed[-max(1,int(limit)):]

===== METHOD Database._meta_examples [2779:2798] =====
2779:     def _meta_examples(self, records, focus_target=None):
2780:         records=list(records or [])
2781:         total=max(1,len(records))
2782:         focus_hour=str((focus_target or {}).get("hora") or "")
2783:         focus_weekday=self._decision_weekday_label((focus_target or {}).get("data")) if focus_target else ""
2784:         examples=[]
2785:         for idx,rec in enumerate(records):
2786:             # Recência suave + contexto do alvo. O peso é aplicado apenas ao
2787:             # treinamento atual; os sinais de cada rodada continuam congelados.
2788:             recency=0.75+0.50*((idx+1)/total)
2789:             weight=recency
2790:             if focus_hour and str(rec.get("target_hora") or "") == focus_hour:
2791:                 weight += 0.60
2792:             if focus_weekday and self._decision_weekday_label(rec.get("target_data")) == focus_weekday:
2793:                 weight += 0.20
2794:             winners=set(int(g) for g in rec.get("result_groups") or [])
2795:             for group in range(1,26):
2796:                 x=self._meta_feature_vector(group,rec.get("signals"),rec.get("historical"),rec.get("base_draw"))
2797:                 examples.append((x,1 if group in winners else 0,weight))
2798:         return examples

===== METHOD Database._meta_temporal_holdout [2800:2837] =====
2800:     def _meta_temporal_holdout(self, records):
2801:         records=list(records or [])
2802:         if len(records) < 24:
2803:             return {"available":False,"rounds":0,"note":"Mínimo de 24 snapshots para holdout temporal preliminar."}
2804:         split=max(16,int(len(records)*0.80))
2805:         if len(records)-split < 4:
2806:             split=len(records)-4
2807:         train,test=records[:split],records[split:]
2808:         model=self._meta_fit_logit(self._meta_examples(train,focus_target=None))
2809:         if not model:
2810:             return {"available":False,"rounds":0,"note":"Modelo não pôde ser ajustado no bloco de treino."}
2811:         hits=[]; random_expected=[]; benchmarks={name:[] for name in ("Reset Cobertura","Puxada Combinada","Similaridade","Histórico Concentrado")}
2812:         for rec in test:
2813:             ranking=[]
2814:             for group in range(1,26):
2815:                 x=self._meta_feature_vector(group,rec.get("signals"),rec.get("historical"),rec.get("base_draw"))
2816:                 ranking.append((self._meta_model_score(model,x),group))
2817:             ranking.sort(key=lambda t:(-t[0],t[1]))
2818:             top={g for _,g in ranking[:5]}
2819:             result=set(int(g) for g in rec.get("result_groups") or [])
2820:             hits.append(len(top & result))
2821:             random_expected.append(5.0*len(result)/25.0)
2822:             for name in ("Reset Cobertura","Puxada Combinada","Similaridade"):
2823:                 groups=set(int(g) for g in ((rec.get("signals") or {}).get(name) or {}).get("groups") or [])
2824:                 benchmarks[name].append(len(groups & result))
2825:             hgroups=set(int(r.get("grupo")) for r in (rec.get("historical") or {}).get("selected") or [])
2826:             benchmarks["Histórico Concentrado"].append(len(hgroups & result))
2827:         n=len(hits)
2828:         return {
2829:             "available":True,"rounds":n,
2830:             "avg_coverage":round(sum(hits)/n,4),
2831:             "pct_2plus":round(sum(v>=2 for v in hits)/n*100.0,2),
2832:             "pct_3plus":round(sum(v>=3 for v in hits)/n*100.0,2),
2833:             "random_expected":round(sum(random_expected)/n,4),
2834:             "uplift_vs_random":round((sum(hits)-sum(random_expected))/n,4),
2835:             "benchmarks":{k:round(sum(v)/len(v),4) if v else None for k,v in benchmarks.items()},
2836:             "note":"Holdout temporal preliminar: bloco final nunca participa do treino. O Walk-Forward completo fica para a fase seguinte.",
2837:         }

===== METHOD Database.meta_walk_forward INICIO [2839:2929] =====
2839:     def meta_walk_forward(self, window=30, progress_callback=None, cancel_event=None):
2840:         """
2841:         Walk-Forward rigoroso do GP-H Meta v0.2, exclusivamente diagnóstico.
2842: 
2843:         Reconstrói sinais históricos com os métodos atuais e cutoff da própria
2844:         extração-base. Para cada rodada avaliada:
2845:           1) usa somente registros anteriores como treino;
2846:           2) reaplica EXATAMENTE o cérebro Meta v0.1 congelado;
2847:           3) prevê Top 5;
2848:           4) só então usa o resultado-alvo para medir desempenho;
2849:           5) avança uma rodada e repete.
2850: 
2851:         Não alimenta o Meta prospectivo, não calibra score, não muda pesos e não

2853:         nativo repetido; cada ajuste pode usar até 160 rodadas anteriores.
2854:         """

2856:             window=max(5,min(60,int(window)))

===== METHOD Database._meta_audit_payload [3310:3325] =====
3310:     def _meta_audit_payload(meta,result_groups):
3311:         meta=meta or {}; result_groups=[int(g) for g in (result_groups or [])]
3312:         groups=[int(g) for g in (meta.get("groups") or [])]

3314:         ranking={int(r.get("grupo")):int(r.get("rank") or 99) for r in (meta.get("ranking") or []) if r.get("grupo")}
3315:         if not meta.get("available") or not groups:
3316:             return {"schema":1,"available":False,"classification":"SEM_DADOS","result_groups":result_groups}
3317:         hits=len(set(groups)&result_set)
3318:         ranks=sorted(ranking[g] for g in result_set if g in ranking)
3319:         return {
3320:             "schema":1,"available":True,"groups":groups,"coverage_hits":hits,
3321:             "first_prize_hit":bool(result_groups and result_groups[0] in set(groups)),
3322:             "result_groups":result_groups,"best_result_rank":ranks[0] if ranks else None,
3323:             "result_ranks":ranks,"random_expected":round(5.0*len(result_set)/25.0,4),
3324:             "classification":f"{hits}/5","status":meta.get("status"),

===== METHOD Database.meta_shadow_summary [3327:3358] =====

3332:                 "AND meta_audit_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(meta_audit_json)<>'' "

3338:             audit=row.get("meta_audit") or {}; meta=row.get("meta") or {}
3339:             if not audit.get("available"): continue
3340:             valid.append((row,audit,meta))
3341:             if len(recent)<12:
3342:                 recent.append({
3343:                     "data":row.get("target_data"),"sorteio":row.get("target_sorteio"),"hora":row.get("target_hora"),

3345:                     "groups":meta.get("groups") or [],
3346:                 })
3347:         n=len(valid)
3348:         if not n:
3349:             return {"rounds":0,"avg_coverage":None,"pct_2plus":None,"pct_3plus":None,"first_rate":None,"random_expected":None,"uplift_vs_random":None,"recent":[]}
3350:         hits=[int(a.get("coverage_hits") or 0) for _,a,_ in valid]
3351:         rnd=[float(a.get("random_expected") or 0.0) for _,a,_ in valid]
3352:         first=[bool(a.get("first_prize_hit")) for _,a,_ in valid]
3353:         return {
3354:             "rounds":n,"avg_coverage":sum(hits)/n,
3355:             "pct_2plus":sum(v>=2 for v in hits)/n*100.0,"pct_3plus":sum(v>=3 for v in hits)/n*100.0,
3356:             "first_rate":sum(first)/n*100.0,"random_expected":sum(rnd)/n,
3357:             "uplift_vs_random":sum(hits)/n-sum(rnd)/n,"recent":recent,
3358:         }

===== METHOD Database.freeze_decision_contextual [3360:3435] =====
3360:     def freeze_decision_contextual(self, snapshot=None, force=False):
3361:         """
3362:         Congela a primeira Decisão Contextual da rodada sem olhar o resultado.
3363: 
3364:         O congelamento automático usa a versão reproduzível sem Walk-Forward de
3365:         sessão. Uma simulação executada depois pode continuar como diagnóstico,
3366:         mas nunca reescreve o líder que será auditado.
3367:         """
3368:         snapshot = snapshot or self.latest_decision_snapshot()
3369:         if not snapshot:
3370:             raise ValueError("Não há snapshot prospectivo para congelar a Decisão Contextual.")
3371: 
3372:         target = {
3373:             "data": snapshot.get("target_data"),
3374:             "sorteio": snapshot.get("target_sorteio"),
3375:             "hora": snapshot.get("target_hora"),
3376:         }
3377:         if not all(target.values()):
3378:             raise ValueError("O snapshot não possui rodada-alvo completa.")
3379: 
3380:         # Regra anti-lookahead absoluta: nem force permite criar uma leitura
3381:         # contextual depois que o resultado-alvo já existe na base.
3382:         if self.get_draw(target["data"], target["sorteio"], target["hora"]):
3383:             return snapshot, False
3384: 
3385:         existing = snapshot.get("contextual") or {}
3386:         if existing and not force:
3387:             return snapshot, False
3388: 
3389:         result = self.decision_contextual_evidence(
3390:             target=target,
3391:             snapshot=snapshot,
3392:             window=120,
3393:             recent_window=12,
3394:             walk_forward=None,
3395:         )
3396:         payload = copy.deepcopy(result or {})
3397:         payload = self.decision_adaptive_context(payload, target=target, limit=120)
3398:         payload = self.decision_operational_recommendation(payload, target=target)
3399:         frozen_at = datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
3400:         best = payload.get("best") or {}
3401:         payload.update({
3402:             "audit_schema": 1,
3403:             "frozen_at": frozen_at,
3404:             "frozen_app_version": APP_VERSION,
3405:             "frozen_mode": "AUTO_SEM_WALK_FORWARD",
3406:             "evidence_leader": best.get("method") if best else None,
3407:         })
3408: 
3409:         with self.connect() as con:
3410:             row = con.execute(
3411:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE id=?",
3412:                 (int(snapshot["id"]),),
3413:             ).fetchone()
3414:             if row is None:
3415:                 raise ValueError("Snapshot de Decisão não encontrado.")
3416:             current = self._decision_row_to_dict(row)
3417:             if (current.get("contextual") or {}) and not force:
3418:                 return current, False
3419:             # Revalida dentro da operação para não aceitar resultado que tenha
3420:             # aparecido entre a leitura e a gravação.
3421:             if self.get_draw(target["data"], target["sorteio"], target["hora"]):
3422:                 return current, False
3423:             con.execute(
3424:                 "UPDATE decision_snapshots SET contextual_json=?, contextual_frozen_at=? WHERE id=?",
3425:                 (
3426:                     json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False),
3427:                     frozen_at,
3428:                     int(snapshot["id"]),
3429:                 ),
3430:             )
3431:             row = con.execute(
3432:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE id=?",
3433:                 (int(snapshot["id"]),),
3434:             ).fetchone()

===== METHOD Database._decision_contextual_audit_payload [3438:3494] =====
3438:     def _decision_contextual_audit_payload(contextual, signals, result_groups):
3439:         """Compara o líder congelado com a cobertura realmente obtida na rodada."""
3440:         contextual = contextual or {}
3441:         signals = signals or {}
3442:         result_groups = [int(g) for g in (result_groups or [])]
3443:         best = contextual.get("best") or {}
3444:         leader = contextual.get("evidence_leader") or best.get("method")
3445:         methods = ("Reset Cobertura", "Puxada Combinada", "Similaridade")
3446: 
3447:         hits = {}
3448:         for name in methods:
3449:             sig = signals.get(name) or {}
3450:             if not sig.get("available"):
3451:                 continue
3452:             value = sig.get("coverage_hits")
3453:             if value is None:
3454:                 continue
3455:             hits[name] = int(value)
3456: 
3457:         base = {
3458:             "schema": 1,
3459:             "leader": leader,
3460:             "context_status": contextual.get("status") or "SEM DADOS",
3461:             "leader_index": float(best.get("index") or 0.0) if best else None,
3462:             "lead_index": float(contextual.get("lead") or 0.0),
3463:             "hits": hits,
3464:             "result_groups": result_groups,
3465:         }
3466:         if not leader or leader not in hits or not hits:
3467:             base.update({
3468:                 "leader_hits": hits.get(leader) if leader else None,
3469:                 "best_hits": max(hits.values()) if hits else None,
3470:                 "best_methods": [],
3471:                 "classification": "SEM_DADOS",
3472:                 "is_best_or_tied": False,
3473:                 "is_sole_best": False,
3474:             })
3475:             return base
3476: 
3477:         best_hits = max(hits.values())
3478:         winners = sorted(name for name, value in hits.items() if value == best_hits)
3479:         leader_hits = int(hits[leader])
3480:         if leader_hits == best_hits and len(winners) == 1:
3481:             classification = "CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA"
3482:         elif leader_hits == best_hits:
3483:             classification = "CORRETA_EMPATE"
3484:         else:
3485:             classification = "INCORRETA"
3486:         base.update({
3487:             "leader_hits": leader_hits,
3488:             "best_hits": int(best_hits),
3489:             "best_methods": winners,
3490:             "classification": classification,
3491:             "is_best_or_tied": leader_hits == best_hits,
3492:             "is_sole_best": classification == "CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA",
3493:         })
3494:         return base

3517:         by_leader = {}

3525:             leader = audit.get("leader") or contextual.get("evidence_leader") or "—"

3529:                 counts["evaluated"] += 1
3530:             elif classification == "CORRETA_EMPATE":
3531:                 counts["tied_best"] += 1
3532:                 counts["evaluated"] += 1
3533:             elif classification == "INCORRETA":
3534:                 counts["incorrect"] += 1
3535:                 counts["evaluated"] += 1
3536:             else:
3537:                 counts["no_data"] += 1
3538: 
3539:             for bucket_map, key in ((by_status, status), (by_leader, leader)):
3540:                 bucket = bucket_map.setdefault(key, {
3541:                     "label": key, "total": 0, "evaluated": 0,
3542:                     "sole_correct": 0, "tied_best": 0, "incorrect": 0,
3543:                 })
3544:                 bucket["total"] += 1
3545:                 if classification in ("CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA", "CORRETA_EMPATE", "INCORRETA"):
3546:                     bucket["evaluated"] += 1
3547:                     if classification == "CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA":
3548:                         bucket["sole_correct"] += 1
3549:                     elif classification == "CORRETA_EMPATE":
3550:                         bucket["tied_best"] += 1
3551:                     else:
3552:                         bucket["incorrect"] += 1
3553:

3556:                     "target_data": row.get("target_data"),
3557:                     "target_sorteio": row.get("target_sorteio"),

3572:         )

===== METHOD Database.decision_confidence_calibration [3599:3761] =====
3599:     def decision_confidence_calibration(self, limit=240, min_bucket=8):
3600:         """
3601:         Etapa B — calibração observacional dos níveis da Decisão Contextual.
3602: 
3603:         Mede somente leituras congeladas ANTES do resultado. O índice continua
3604:         sendo evidência (0–100), não probabilidade. Esta rotina não altera pesos,
3605:         limiares, rótulos nem qualquer método; apenas verifica se níveis mais fortes
3606:         vêm acompanhados de melhor desempenho prospectivo.
3607:         """
3608:         try:
3609:             limit=max(1,min(5000,int(limit)))
3610:         except Exception:
3611:             limit=240
3612:         try:
3613:             min_bucket=max(3,min(50,int(min_bucket)))
3614:         except Exception:
3615:             min_bucket=8
3616: 
3617:         with self.connect() as con:
3618:             rows=con.execute(
3619:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots "
3620:                 "WHERE status='AUDITADO' AND contextual_json IS NOT NULL "
3621:                 "AND TRIM(contextual_json)<>'' AND contextual_audit_json IS NOT NULL "
3622:                 "AND TRIM(contextual_audit_json)<>'' "
3623:                 "ORDER BY target_data DESC,target_hora DESC,id DESC LIMIT ?",
3624:                 (limit,),
3625:             ).fetchall()
3626: 
3627:         parsed=[self._decision_row_to_dict(row) for row in rows]
3628:         levels=("AMOSTRA INSUFICIENTE","BAIXA","MODERADA","FORTE")
3629:         buckets={level:[] for level in levels}
3630:         all_valid=[]
3631: 
3632:         for row in parsed:
3633:             audit=row.get("contextual_audit") or {}
3634:             contextual=row.get("contextual") or {}
3635:             classification=audit.get("classification") or "SEM_DADOS"
3636:             if classification not in ("CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA","CORRETA_EMPATE","INCORRETA"):
3637:                 continue
3638:             status=str(audit.get("context_status") or contextual.get("status") or "SEM DADOS").upper()
3639:             best=contextual.get("best") or {}
3640:             idx=audit.get("leader_index")
3641:             if idx is None:
3642:                 idx=best.get("index")
3643:             lead=audit.get("lead_index")
3644:             if lead is None:
3645:                 lead=contextual.get("lead")
3646:             rec={
3647:                 "status":status,
3648:                 "success":classification in ("CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA","CORRETA_EMPATE"),
3649:                 "exclusive":classification=="CORRETA_EXCLUSIVA",
3650:                 "incorrect":classification=="INCORRETA",
3651:                 "leader_index":float(idx) if idx is not None else None,
3652:                 "lead_index":float(lead) if lead is not None else None,
3653:                 "leader_hits":int(audit.get("leader_hits")) if audit.get("leader_hits") is not None else None,
3654:                 "classification":classification,
3655:                 "target_data":row.get("target_data"),
3656:                 "target_sorteio":row.get("target_sorteio"),
3657:                 "target_hora":row.get("target_hora"),
3658:             }
3659:             all_valid.append(rec)
3660:             buckets.setdefault(status,[]).append(rec)
3661: 
3662:         def wilson(successes,n,z=1.96):
3663:             if n<=0:
3664:                 return (None,None)
3665:             p=successes/n
3666:             den=1.0+(z*z)/n
3667:             center=(p+(z*z)/(2*n))/den
3668:             margin=(z*((p*(1-p)/n+(z*z)/(4*n*n))**0.5))/den
3669:             return (max(0.0,center-margin)*100.0,min(1.0,center+margin)*100.0)
3670: 
3671:         def mean(values):
3672:             values=[float(v) for v in values if v is not None]
3673:             return sum(values)/len(values) if values else None
3674: 
3675:         ordered_levels=list(levels)+sorted(k for k in buckets if k not in levels)
3676:         out=[]
3677:         for level in ordered_levels:
3678:             values=buckets.get(level) or []
3679:             if not values and level not in levels:
3680:                 continue
3681:             n=len(values)
3682:             success=sum(1 for r in values if r["success"])
3683:             sole=sum(1 for r in values if r["exclusive"])
3684:             incorrect=sum(1 for r in values if r["incorrect"])
3685:             low,high=wilson(success,n)
3686:             out.append({
3687:                 "level":level,"rounds":n,
3688:                 "best_or_tied":success,
3689:                 "best_or_tied_rate":success/n*100.0 if n else None,
3690:                 "sole_correct":sole,
3691:                 "sole_correct_rate":sole/n*100.0 if n else None,
3692:                 "incorrect":incorrect,
3693:                 "incorrect_rate":incorrect/n*100.0 if n else None,
3694:                 "ci95_low":low,"ci95_high":high,
3695:                 "avg_leader_index":mean(r["leader_index"] for r in values),
3696:                 "avg_lead_index":mean(r["lead_index"] for r in values),
3697:                 "avg_leader_hits":mean(r["leader_hits"] for r in values),
3698:                 "sample_status":"SUFICIENTE" if n>=min_bucket else ("PEQUENA" if n else "SEM DADOS"),
3699:             })
3700: 
3701:         evaluated=len(all_valid)
3702:         successes=sum(1 for r in all_valid if r["success"])
3703:         overall_low,overall_high=wilson(successes,evaluated)
3704: 
3705:         # Correlação descritiva índice × desfecho binário. Não é causalidade,
3706:         # não é probabilidade e não recebe significância estatística artificial.
3707:         pairs=[(r["leader_index"],1.0 if r["success"] else 0.0) for r in all_valid if r["leader_index"] is not None]
3708:         corr=None
3709:         if len(pairs)>=8:
3710:             xs=[p[0] for p in pairs]; ys=[p[1] for p in pairs]
3711:             mx=sum(xs)/len(xs); my=sum(ys)/len(ys)
3712:             vx=sum((x-mx)**2 for x in xs); vy=sum((y-my)**2 for y in ys)
3713:             if vx>0 and vy>0:
3714:                 corr=sum((x-mx)*(y-my) for x,y in pairs)/(vx*vy)**0.5
3715: 
3716:         by_level={r["level"]:r for r in out}
3717:         check=[]
3718:         for level in ("BAIXA","MODERADA","FORTE"):
3719:             row=by_level.get(level) or {}
3720:             if int(row.get("rounds") or 0)>=min_bucket and row.get("best_or_tied_rate") is not None:
3721:                 check.append((level,float(row["best_or_tied_rate"])))
3722:         monotonic=None
3723:         if len(check)>=2:
3724:             monotonic=all(check[i][1] <= check[i+1][1]+1e-12 for i in range(len(check)-1))
3725: 
3726:         if evaluated < 20 or len(check) < 2:
3727:             verdict="EM FORMAÇÃO"
3728:             note=(
3729:                 f"Ainda há pouca amostra para julgar a escala de confiança: {evaluated} decisão(ões) avaliável(is) "
3730:                 f"e {len(check)} nível(is) com pelo menos {min_bucket} casos."
3731:             )
3732:         elif monotonic:
3733:             verdict="ORDEM COERENTE"
3734:             note=(
3735:                 "Nos níveis com amostra mínima, a taxa de melhor/empate não diminuiu ao passar de BAIXA para MODERADA/FORTE. "
3736:                 "Isso apoia a ordenação atual, mas ainda não transforma o índice em probabilidade."
3737:             )
3738:         else:
3739:             verdict="ORDEM AINDA NÃO COERENTE"
3740:             note=(
3741:                 "Os níveis com amostra mínima ainda não mostram crescimento ordenado da taxa de melhor/empate. "
3742:                 "A Etapa B apenas registra isso; nenhum limiar é alterado automaticamente."
3743:             )
3744: 
3745:         strong=by_level.get("FORTE") or {}
3746:         low=by_level.get("BAIXA") or {}
3747:         strong_vs_low=None
3748:         if int(strong.get("rounds") or 0)>=min_bucket and int(low.get("rounds") or 0)>=min_bucket:
3749:             strong_vs_low=float(strong.get("best_or_tied_rate") or 0)-float(low.get("best_or_tied_rate") or 0)
3750: 
3751:         return {
3752:             "evaluated":evaluated,"best_or_tied":successes,
3753:             "best_or_tied_rate":successes/evaluated*100.0 if evaluated else None,
3754:             "overall_ci95_low":overall_low,"overall_ci95_high":overall_high,
3755:             "levels":out,"min_bucket":min_bucket,
3756:             "sufficient_levels":len(check),"monotonic":monotonic,
3757:             "index_outcome_corr":corr,"strong_vs_low_pp":strong_vs_low,
3758:             "verdict":verdict,"note":note,
3759:             "primary_metric":"melhor_ou_empatada",
3760:             "lookahead_safe":True,"changes_decision":False,
3761:         }

===== METHOD Database.decision_adaptive_context INICIO [3764:3854] =====
3764:     def decision_adaptive_context(
3765:         self, contextual, target=None, limit=120, min_total=30,
3766:         min_method_rounds=20, max_adjustment=3.0,
3767:     ):
3768:         """
3769:         Etapa C — Decisão Adaptativa v0.1.
3770: 
3771:         Usa SOMENTE auditorias contextuais já encerradas antes da rodada-alvo
3772:         para aplicar uma correção pequena e regularizada aos índices dos três
3773:         métodos. A camada não toca nas fórmulas de Reset/Puxada/Similaridade,
3774:         não altera o GP-H Meta e não muda o Reset + 3+1 oficial.
3775: 
3776:         Princípios:
3777:         - sem look-ahead: nenhuma rodada igual/posterior ao alvo entra;
3778:         - ativação somente com amostra mínima pareada;
3779:         - ajuste máximo por método limitado a +/- max_adjustment pontos;
3780:         - histórico geral domina; mesmo horário entra apenas como refinamento;
3781:         - calibração de nível pode apenas REBAIXAR confiança incoerente, nunca
3782:           promover confiança artificialmente;
3783:         - decisão-base permanece gravada dentro do payload para auditoria A/B.
3784:         """
3785:         contextual = copy.deepcopy(contextual or {})
3786:         target = dict(target or contextual.get("target") or {})
3787:         methods = ("Reset Cobertura", "Puxada Combinada", "Similaridade")
3788:         rows = [copy.deepcopy(r) for r in (contextual.get("rows") or []) if r.get("method") in methods]
3789:         base_best = copy.deepcopy(contextual.get("best") or (rows[0] if rows else {}))
3790:         base_leader = base_best.get("method")
3791:         base_status = contextual.get("status") or "SEM DADOS"
3792:         base_lead = float(contextual.get("lead") or 0.0)
3793:         base_recommendation = contextual.get("recommendation") or ""
3794: 
3795:         try:
3796:             limit = max(20, min(500, int(limit)))
3797:         except Exception:
3798:             limit = 120
3799:         try:
3800:             min_total = max(20, min(200, int(min_total)))
3801:         except Exception:
3802:             min_total = 30
3803:         try:
3804:             min_method_rounds = max(8, min(100, int(min_method_rounds)))
3805:         except Exception:
3806:             min_method_rounds = 20
3807:         try:
3808:             max_adjustment = max(0.5, min(5.0, float(max_adjustment)))
3809:         except Exception:
3810:             max_adjustment = 3.0
3811: 
3812:         def before_target(row):
3813:             td = str(target.get("data") or "")
3814:             th = str(target.get("hora") or "")
3815:             rd = str(row.get("target_data") or "")
3816:             rh = str(row.get("target_hora") or "")
3817:             if not td:
3818:                 return True
3819:             if rd < td:
3820:                 return True
3821:             if rd > td:
3822:                 return False
3823:             if th and rh:
3824:                 return rh < th
3825:             return False
3826: 
3827:         with self.connect() as con:
3828:             raw_rows = con.execute(
3829:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE status='AUDITADO' "
3830:                 "AND contextual_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(contextual_json)<>'' "
3831:                 "AND contextual_audit_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(contextual_audit_json)<>'' "
3832:                 "ORDER BY target_data DESC,target_hora DESC,id DESC LIMIT 500"
3833:             ).fetchall()
3834: 
3835:         prior = []
3836:         for raw in raw_rows:
3837:             row = self._decision_row_to_dict(raw)
3838:             if not before_target(row):
3839:                 continue
3840:             audit = row.get("contextual_audit") or {}
3841:             hits = audit.get("hits") or {}
3842:             usable = {}
3843:             for name in methods:
3844:                 if name not in hits or hits.get(name) is None:
3845:                     continue
3846:                 try:
3847:                     usable[name] = int(hits.get(name))
3848:                 except Exception:
3849:                     pass
3850:             if len(usable) < 2:
3851:                 continue
3852:             prior.append((row, usable))
3853:             if len(prior) >= limit:
3854:                 break

4073:         """

===== METHOD Database.decision_operational_audit_summary [4187:4283] =====
4187:     def decision_operational_audit_summary(self, limit=120):
4188:         """Audita apenas recomendações da Etapa D realmente congeladas antes do resultado."""
4189:         try:
4190:             limit = max(1, min(1000, int(limit)))
4191:         except Exception:
4192:             limit = 120
4193:         with self.connect() as con:
4194:             rows = con.execute(
4195:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE status='AUDITADO' "
4196:                 "AND contextual_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(contextual_json)<>'' "
4197:                 "AND contextual_audit_json IS NOT NULL AND TRIM(contextual_audit_json)<>'' "
4198:                 "ORDER BY target_data DESC,target_hora DESC,id DESC LIMIT ?",
4199:                 (limit,),
4200:             ).fetchall()
4201: 
4202:         evaluated = actionable = correct = sole_best = errors = 0
4203:         no_clear = insufficient = 0
4204:         selected_hits = []
4205:         regrets = []
4206:         recent = []
4207:         action_counts = {}
4208: 
4209:         for raw in rows:
4210:             row = self._decision_row_to_dict(raw)
4211:             contextual = row.get("contextual") or {}
4212:             operational = contextual.get("operational") or {}
4213:             if int(operational.get("schema") or 0) != 1:
4214:                 continue
4215:             audit = row.get("contextual_audit") or {}
4216:             hits = audit.get("hits") or {}
4217:             if not hits:
4218:                 continue
4219: 
4220:             evaluated += 1
4221:             code = str(operational.get("action_code") or "SEM_DADOS")
4222:             action_counts[code] = int(action_counts.get(code) or 0) + 1
4223:             if code == "SEM_VANTAGEM_CLARA":
4224:                 no_clear += 1
4225:             elif code == "EVIDENCIA_INSUFICIENTE":
4226:                 insufficient += 1
4227: 
4228:             is_actionable = bool(operational.get("actionable"))
4229:             method = operational.get("recommended_method")
4230:             result = None
4231:             chosen_hits = None
4232:             best_hits = None
4233:             if is_actionable and method in hits:
4234:                 try:
4235:                     chosen_hits = int(hits[method])
4236:                     best_hits = max(int(v) for v in hits.values())
4237:                 except Exception:
4238:                     chosen_hits = best_hits = None
4239:                 if chosen_hits is not None and best_hits is not None:
4240:                     actionable += 1
4241:                     selected_hits.append(chosen_hits)
4242:                     regrets.append(best_hits - chosen_hits)
4243:                     winners = [name for name, value in hits.items() if int(value) == best_hits]
4244:                     if chosen_hits == best_hits:
4245:                         correct += 1
4246:                         if len(winners) == 1:
4247:                             sole_best += 1
4248:                         result = "MELHOR/EMPATE"
4249:                     else:
4250:                         errors += 1
4251:                         result = "ABAIXO_DO_MELHOR"
4252: 
4253:             if len(recent) < 12:
4254:                 recent.append({
4255:                     "data": row.get("target_data"),
4256:                     "sorteio": row.get("target_sorteio"),
4257:                     "hora": row.get("target_hora"),
4258:                     "action_code": code,
4259:                     "method": method,
4260:                     "hits": chosen_hits,
4261:                     "best_hits": best_hits,
4262:                     "result": result,
4263:                 })
4264: 
4265:         return {
4266:             "evaluated": evaluated,
4267:             "actionable": actionable,
4268:             "correct": correct,
4269:             "sole_best": sole_best,
4270:             "errors": errors,
4271:             "no_clear": no_clear,
4272:             "insufficient": insufficient,
4273:             "best_or_tied_rate": (correct / actionable * 100.0) if actionable else None,
4274:             "sole_best_rate": (sole_best / actionable * 100.0) if actionable else None,
4275:             "avg_selected_coverage": (sum(selected_hits) / len(selected_hits)) if selected_hits else None,
4276:             "avg_regret": (sum(regrets) / len(regrets)) if regrets else None,
4277:             "action_counts": action_counts,
4278:             "recent": recent,
4279:             "lookahead_safe": True,
4280:             "note": (
4281:                 "Somente recomendações D congeladas prospectivamente são avaliadas. Rodadas em que a D recomendou não priorizar não viram acerto/erro artificial."
4282:             ),
4283:         }

4336:                 neutral += 1
4337:             if len(recent) < 12:
4338:                 recent.append({
4339:                     "data": row.get("target_data"),
4340:                     "sorteio": row.get("target_sorteio"),
4341:                     "hora": row.get("target_hora"),
4342:                     "active": bool(adaptive.get("active")),
4343:                     "base_leader": base_leader,
4344:                     "final_leader": final_leader,
4345:                     "base_hits": bh,
4346:                     "final_hits": ah,
4347:                     "delta": ah - bh,
4348:                 })
4349: 
4350:         return {
4351:             "evaluated": evaluated,
4352:             "active_rounds": active_rounds,
4353:             "changed": changed,
4354:             "improved": improved,
4355:             "harmed": harmed,
4356:             "neutral": neutral,
4357:             "base_best_or_tied_rate": (base_success / evaluated * 100.0) if evaluated else None,
4358:             "adaptive_best_or_tied_rate": (adaptive_success / evaluated * 100.0) if evaluated else None,
4359:             "avg_hit_delta": (sum(delta_hits) / len(delta_hits)) if delta_hits else None,
4360:             "recent": recent,
4361:             "lookahead_safe": True,
4362:             "note": "Somente snapshots congelados já com Etapa C; nenhuma rodada antiga é reconstruída.",
4363:         }

===== METHOD Database.audit_decision_snapshots [4365:4420] =====
4365:     def audit_decision_snapshots(self):
4366:         """Audita snapshots e, quando existente, a própria decisão contextual congelada."""
4367:         changed = 0
4368:         with self.connect() as con:

4370:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE status='PENDENTE' ORDER BY id"
4371:             ).fetchall()

4373:                 target = self.get_draw(row["target_data"], row["target_sorteio"], row["target_hora"])
4374:                 if not target or len(target.get("prizes") or []) < 5:
4375:                     continue
4376:                 result_groups = [int(p["grupo"]) for p in target["prizes"]]
4377:                 result_set = set(result_groups)
4378:                 try:
4379:                     signals = json.loads(row["signals_json"] or "{}")
4380:                 except Exception:
4381:                     signals = {}

4384:                     sig["coverage_hits"] = len(set(groups) & result_set)
4385:                     if sig.get("positional") and len(groups) >= 5:
4386:                         sig["position_hits"] = sum(
4387:                             1 for i in range(5) if int(groups[i]) == int(result_groups[i])
4388:                         )
4389:                     else:
4390:                         sig["position_hits"] = None
4391:

4396:                 except Exception:
4397:                     contextual = {}
4398:                 contextual_audit = (
4399:                     self._decision_contextual_audit_payload(contextual, signals, result_groups)
4400:                     if contextual else None
4401:                 )
4402:                 try:
4403:                     meta = json.loads(row["meta_json"] or "{}")
4404:                 except Exception:

4410:                     (

4413:                         json.dumps(meta_audit, ensure_ascii=False) if meta_audit else None,
4414:                         json.dumps(result_groups),
4415:                         datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
4416:                         int(row["id"]),
4417:                     ),

===== METHOD Database._shadow_row_to_dict [4447:4458] =====
4447:     def _shadow_row_to_dict(self, row):
4448:         if row is None:
4449:             return None
4450:         d = dict(row)
4451:         for field in ("payload_json", "result_json"):
4452:             raw = d.get(field)
4453:             key = field.replace("_json", "")
4454:             try:
4455:                 d[key] = json.loads(raw) if raw else {}
4456:             except Exception:
4457:                 d[key] = {}
4458:         return d
