===== METHOD Database.audit_shadow_snapshots [4629:4690] =====
4629:     def audit_shadow_snapshots(self):
4630:         """Audita silenciosamente leituras sombra cujo alvo já possui 1º–5º."""
4631:         changed = 0
4632:         with self.connect() as con:
4633:             rows = con.execute(
4634:                 "SELECT * FROM shadow_snapshots WHERE status='PENDENTE' ORDER BY id"
4635:             ).fetchall()
4636:             for row in rows:
4637:                 target = self.get_draw(row["target_data"], row["target_sorteio"], row["target_hora"])
4638:                 prizes = (target or {}).get("prizes") or []
4639:                 if len(prizes) < 5:
4640:                     continue
4641:                 target_centenas = [str(p.get("centena") or "").zfill(3) for p in prizes]
4642:                 target_groups = [int(p["grupo"]) for p in prizes]
4643:                 result_set = set(target_groups)
4644:                 try:
4645:                     payload = json.loads(row["payload_json"] or "{}")
4646:                 except Exception:
4647:                     payload = {}
4648: 
4649:                 for rec in (payload.get("centena") or {}).values():
4650:                     nums = [str(x).zfill(3) for x in (rec.get("numbers") or [])]
4651:                     nums1 = [str(x).zfill(3) for x in (rec.get("numbers_1") or rec.get("numbers") or [])]
4652:                     matches = sorted(set(nums) & set(target_centenas))
4653:                     first_match = bool(target_centenas and target_centenas[0] in set(nums1))
4654:                     rec["audited"] = True
4655:                     rec["hit_count"] = len(matches)
4656:                     rec["win"] = bool(matches)
4657:                     rec["matched_numbers"] = matches
4658:                     # Para colocação 1º, usa o jogo que teria sido realmente gerado
4659:                     # com ranking numérico de 1º prêmio (exceto 3+1, cuja fórmula interna é fixa).
4660:                     rec["first_prize_hit"] = first_match
4661:                     rec["matched_first_number"] = target_centenas[0] if first_match else None
4662: 
4663:                 for rec in (payload.get("bichos") or {}).values():
4664:                     groups = [int(g) for g in (rec.get("groups") or [])]
4665:                     rec["audited"] = True
4666:                     rec["coverage_hits"] = len(set(groups) & result_set)
4667:                     rec["first_prize_hit"] = bool(target_groups and target_groups[0] in set(groups))
4668: 
4669:                 for rec in (payload.get("seca_1p") or {}).values():
4670:                     groups = [int(g) for g in (rec.get("groups") or [])]
4671:                     rec["audited"] = True
4672:                     rec["first_prize_hit"] = bool(target_groups and target_groups[0] in set(groups))
4673:                     rec["coverage_hits"] = int(rec["first_prize_hit"])
4674: 
4675:                 result = {
4676:                     "centenas": target_centenas,
4677:                     "groups": target_groups,
4678:                     "first_group": target_groups[0] if target_groups else None,
4679:                 }
4680:                 con.execute(
4681:                     "UPDATE shadow_snapshots SET status='AUDITADO',payload_json=?,result_json=?,audited_at=? WHERE id=?",
4682:                     (
4683:                         json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False),
4684:                         json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False),
4685:                         datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
4686:                         int(row["id"]),
4687:                     ),
4688:                 )
4689:                 changed += 1
4690:         return changed

===== METHOD Database._shadow_evidence_label [4693:4701] =====
4693:     def _shadow_evidence_label(rounds, lead=0.0, recent_drop=False):
4694:         rounds = int(rounds or 0)
4695:         if rounds < 8:
4696:             return "AMOSTRA INSUFICIENTE"
4697:         if rounds >= 20 and lead >= 8.0 and not recent_drop:
4698:             return "FORTE"
4699:         if rounds >= 12 and lead >= 4.0 and not recent_drop:
4700:             return "MODERADA"
4701:         return "BAIXA"

===== METHOD Database.shadow_recommendation [4703:4804] =====
4703:     def shadow_recommendation(self, target=None, window=120, scope="1º–5º"):
4704:         """
4705:         Recomendação prospectiva por HORÁRIO baseada apenas em sombras auditadas.
4706:         Não retorna mensagens retrospectivas por rodada.
4707:         """
4708:         target = dict(target or self.next_operational_target() or {})
4709:         scope = "1º" if str(scope) == "1º" else "1º–5º"
4710:         hour = str(target.get("hora") or "")
4711:         with self.connect() as con:
4712:             rows = con.execute(
4713:                 "SELECT * FROM shadow_snapshots WHERE status='AUDITADO' AND target_hora=? "
4714:                 "ORDER BY target_data DESC,id DESC LIMIT ?",
4715:                 (hour, max(1, int(window))),
4716:             ).fetchall()
4717:         parsed = [self._shadow_row_to_dict(r) for r in rows]
4718: 
4719:         def centena_stats(name):
4720:             vals=[]
4721:             hits=[]
4722:             for row in parsed:
4723:                 rec=((row.get("payload") or {}).get("centena") or {}).get(name)
4724:                 if not rec or not rec.get("audited"):
4725:                     continue
4726:                 if scope == "1º":
4727:                     vals.append(bool(rec.get("first_prize_hit")))
4728:                     hits.append(1 if rec.get("first_prize_hit") else 0)
4729:                 else:
4730:                     vals.append(bool(rec.get("win")))
4731:                     hits.append(int(rec.get("hit_count") or 0))
4732:             n=len(vals)
4733:             rate=(sum(vals)/n*100.0) if n else 0.0
4734:             recent=vals[:min(12,n)]
4735:             recent_rate=(sum(recent)/len(recent)*100.0) if recent else rate
4736:             return {"name":name,"rounds":n,"win_rate":rate,"recent_rate":recent_rate,
4737:                     "avg_hits":(sum(hits)/n if n else 0.0)}
4738: 
4739:         centena_names=("3+1","Reset + Histórica","Puxada + Histórica","Similaridade + Histórica")
4740:         cent = [centena_stats(n) for n in centena_names]
4741:         cent = [r for r in cent if r["rounds"]]
4742:         cent.sort(key=lambda r:(-r["win_rate"],-r["recent_rate"],-r["avg_hits"],r["name"]))
4743:         cent_rec={"status":"SEM DADOS","best":None,"rows":cent}
4744:         if cent:
4745:             best=cent[0]
4746:             second=cent[1] if len(cent)>1 else None
4747:             lead=best["win_rate"]-(second["win_rate"] if second else 0.0)
4748:             drop=best["recent_rate"]+12.0 < best["win_rate"]
4749:             cent_rec={
4750:                 "status":self._shadow_evidence_label(best["rounds"],lead,drop),
4751:                 "best":best,"lead":lead,"rows":cent,
4752:             }
4753: 
4754:         # Bichos: compara Reset, Puxada e Similaridade pela cobertura média Top 5.
4755:         bstats=[]
4756:         for name in ("Reset Cobertura","Puxada Combinada","Similaridade"):
4757:             vals=[]
4758:             for row in parsed:
4759:                 rec=((row.get("payload") or {}).get("bichos") or {}).get(name)
4760:                 if rec and rec.get("audited"):
4761:                     if scope == "1º":
4762:                         vals.append(1 if rec.get("first_prize_hit") else 0)
4763:                     else:
4764:                         vals.append(int(rec.get("coverage_hits") or 0))
4765:             if vals:
4766:                 recent=vals[:min(12,len(vals))]
4767:                 bstats.append({
4768:                     "name":name,"rounds":len(vals),"avg_coverage":sum(vals)/len(vals),
4769:                     "recent_avg":sum(recent)/len(recent),
4770:                 })
4771:         bstats.sort(key=lambda r:(-r["avg_coverage"],-r["recent_avg"],r["name"]))
4772:         bicho_rec={"status":"SEM DADOS","best":None,"rows":bstats}
4773:         if bstats:
4774:             best=bstats[0]
4775:             second=bstats[1] if len(bstats)>1 else None
4776:             lead=(best["avg_coverage"]-(second["avg_coverage"] if second else 0.0))*20.0
4777:             drop=best["recent_avg"]+0.45 < best["avg_coverage"]
4778:             bicho_rec={
4779:                 "status":self._shadow_evidence_label(best["rounds"],lead,drop),
4780:                 "best":best,"lead":lead,"rows":bstats,
4781:             }
4782: 
4783:         # Seca do 1º prêmio: objetivo específico e separado.
4784:         dry_vals=[]
4785:         for row in parsed:
4786:             rec=((row.get("payload") or {}).get("seca_1p") or {}).get("Seca do Dia 1º")
4787:             if rec and rec.get("audited"):
4788:                 dry_vals.append(bool(rec.get("first_prize_hit")))
4789:         dry={"status":"SEM DADOS","rounds":0,"hit_rate":0.0}
4790:         if dry_vals:
4791:             n=len(dry_vals)
4792:             rate=sum(dry_vals)/n*100.0
4793:             recent=dry_vals[:min(12,n)]
4794:             recent_rate=sum(recent)/len(recent)*100.0
4795:             dry={
4796:                 "status":self._shadow_evidence_label(n, 5.0, recent_rate+12.0 < rate),
4797:                 "rounds":n,"hit_rate":rate,"recent_rate":recent_rate,
4798:             }
4799: 
4800:         return {
4801:             "target":target,"hour":hour,"scope":scope,"audited_rounds":len(parsed),
4802:             "centena":cent_rec,"bichos":bicho_rec,"seca_1p":dry,
4803:             "note":"Evidência prospectiva; não é probabilidade de acerto.",
4804:         }

===== METHOD Database.shadow_lab_status [4806:4841] =====
4806:     def shadow_lab_status(self, target=None):
4807:         """Resumo operacional do Laboratório Sombra sem expor arrependimento por rodada."""
4808:         target = dict(target or self.next_operational_target() or {})
4809:         hour = str(target.get("hora") or "")

4819:                     "MAX(CASE WHEN status='AUDITADO' THEN audited_at END) last_audited "

4821:                     (hour,),

4824:                     audited = int(row["audited"] or 0)

===== METHOD Database.decision_explain_group [4843:4866] =====
4843:     def decision_explain_group(self, snapshot, group):
4844:         group = int(group)
4845:         details = []
4846:         for method, sig in (snapshot or {}).get("signals", {}).items():
4847:             groups = [int(g) for g in (sig.get("groups") or [])]
4848:             if group in groups:
4849:                 rank = groups.index(group) + 1
4850:                 extra = ""
4851:                 if sig.get("kind") == "reset":
4852:                     scores = sig.get("scores") or []
4853:                     if rank <= len(scores):
4854:                         extra = f" • score {scores[rank-1]:.2f}%"
4855:                 elif sig.get("kind") == "pull":
4856:                     counts = sig.get("source_counts") or []
4857:                     if rank <= len(counts):
4858:                         extra = f" • {counts[rank-1]} fonte(s)"
4859:                 elif sig.get("kind") == "similarity":
4860:                     shares = sig.get("weighted_shares") or []
4861:                     if rank <= len(shares):
4862:                         extra = f" • voto ponderado {shares[rank-1]:.1f}%"
4863:                 details.append(f"{method}: posição {rank}{extra}")
4864:             else:
4865:                 details.append(f"{method}: fora do Top 5")
4866:         return details

===== METHOD Database.build_sync_snapshot [5463:5557] =====
5463:     def build_sync_snapshot(self, profile, visual_settings=None):
5464:         """Exporta o estado sincronizável sem expor IDs locais de relações."""
5465:         profile = profile or {}
5466:         with self.connect() as con:
5467:             results = [dict(r) for r in con.execute(
5468:                 "SELECT * FROM resultados ORDER BY data, hora, sorteio, premio"
5469:             ).fetchall()]
5470:             for r in results:
5471:                 r.pop("id", None)
5472:

5474:                 "SELECT * FROM bilhetes ORDER BY id"
5475:             ).fetchall()]
5476:             ticket_uid_by_id = {}
5477:             for t in tickets:
5478:                 ticket_uid_by_id[int(t["id"])] = t.get("sync_uid")
5479:                 t.pop("id", None)
5480: 
5481:             games = [dict(r) for r in con.execute(
5482:                 "SELECT * FROM jogos_congelados ORDER BY id"
5483:             ).fetchall()]
5484:             exported_games = []
5485:             for g in games:
5486:                 local_id = int(g["id"])
5487:                 ticket_id = g.get("bilhete_id")
5488:                 g["bilhete_sync_uid"] = (
5489:                     ticket_uid_by_id.get(int(ticket_id)) if ticket_id else None
5490:                 )
5491:                 g.pop("id", None)
5492:                 g.pop("bilhete_id", None)
5493:                 items = [dict(r) for r in con.execute(
5494:                     "SELECT * FROM jogos_itens WHERE jogo_id=? ORDER BY ordem, id",
5495:                     (local_id,),
5496:                 ).fetchall()]
5497:                 for item in items:
5498:                     item.pop("id", None)
5499:                     item.pop("jogo_id", None)
5500:                 g["items"] = items
5501:                 exported_games.append(g)
5502: 
5503:             meta_rows = [
5504:                 dict(r)
5505:                 for r in con.execute(
5506:                     "SELECT chave, valor FROM meta "
5507:                     "WHERE chave LIKE 'quote_%' "
5508:                     "OR chave IN ('bankroll_initial','bankroll_start_date') "
5509:                     "ORDER BY chave"
5510:                 ).fetchall()
5511:             ]
5512: 
5513:             decision_rows = [dict(r) for r in con.execute(
5514:                 "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots ORDER BY id"
5515:             ).fetchall()]
5516:             for row in decision_rows:
5517:                 row.pop("id", None)
5518: 
5519:             shadow_rows = [dict(r) for r in con.execute(
5520:                 "SELECT * FROM shadow_snapshots ORDER BY id"
5521:             ).fetchall()]
5522:             for row in shadow_rows:
5523:                 row.pop("id", None)
5524: 
5525:         now = datetime.now().isoformat(timespec="seconds")
5526:         snapshot = {
5527:             "format_version": SYNC_FORMAT_VERSION,
5528:             "app_version": APP_VERSION,
5529:             "profile_code": normalize_profile_code(profile.get("profile_code")),
5530:             "profile_name": profile.get("profile_name") or "Perfil GP-H",
5531:             "generated_at": now,
5532:             "device": {
5533:                 "device_id": profile.get("device_id"),
5534:                 "device_name": profile.get("device_name") or default_device_name(),
5535:                 "last_login_at": profile.get("last_login_at"),
5536:             },
5537:             "visual_settings": dict(visual_settings or {}),
5538:             "meta": meta_rows,
5539:             "resultados": results,
5540:             "bilhetes": tickets,
5541:             "jogos": exported_games,
5542:             "decision_snapshots": decision_rows,
5543:             "shadow_snapshots": shadow_rows,
5544:         }
5545:         content = {
5546:             "visual_settings": snapshot["visual_settings"],
5547:             "meta": meta_rows,
5548:             "resultados": results,
5549:             "bilhetes": tickets,
5550:             "jogos": exported_games,
5551:             "decision_snapshots": decision_rows,
5552:             "shadow_snapshots": shadow_rows,
5553:         }
5554:         snapshot["content_hash"] = hashlib.sha256(
5555:             json.dumps(content, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
5556:         ).hexdigest()

===== METHOD Database._merge_sync_meta [5607:5625] =====
5607:     def _merge_sync_meta(self, rows):
5608:         changed = 0
5609:         allowed = {"bankroll_initial", "bankroll_start_date"}
5610:         with self.connect() as con:
5611:             for row in rows or []:
5612:                 key = str(row.get("chave") or "")
5613:                 if not (key.startswith("quote_") or key in allowed):
5614:                     continue
5615:                 value = row.get("valor")
5616:                 current = con.execute(
5617:                     "SELECT valor FROM meta WHERE chave=?", (key,)
5618:                 ).fetchone()
5619:                 if current is None or str(current[0]) != str(value):
5620:                     con.execute(
5621:                         "INSERT OR REPLACE INTO meta(chave, valor) VALUES(?, ?)",
5622:                         (key, value),
5623:                     )
5624:                     changed += 1
5625:         return changed

===== METHOD Database._merge_sync_decision_snapshots [5776:5860] =====
5776:     def _merge_sync_decision_snapshots(self, rows):
5777:         added = updated = 0
5778:         if not rows:
5779:             return added, updated
5780:         cols_allowed = {
5781:             "created_at","base_data","base_sorteio","base_hora","target_data","target_sorteio","target_hora",
5782:             "status","confidence_score","confidence_label","recommendation","components_json","signals_json",

5786:         }
5787:         with self.connect() as con:
5788:             for remote in rows:
5789:                 key = (
5790:                     remote.get("base_data"), remote.get("base_sorteio"), remote.get("base_hora"),
5791:                     remote.get("target_data"), remote.get("target_sorteio"), remote.get("target_hora"),
5792:                 )
5793:                 if not all(key):
5794:                     continue
5795:                 local = con.execute(
5796:                     "SELECT * FROM decision_snapshots WHERE base_data=? AND base_sorteio=? AND base_hora=? "
5797:                     "AND target_data=? AND target_sorteio=? AND target_hora=?", key
5798:                 ).fetchone()
5799:                 if local is None:
5800:                     data = {k: remote.get(k) for k in cols_allowed if k in remote}
5801:                     keys = list(data)
5802:                     con.execute(
5803:                         f"INSERT INTO decision_snapshots ({','.join(keys)}) VALUES ({','.join('?' for _ in keys)})",
5804:                         [data[k] for k in keys],
5805:                     )
5806:                     added += 1
5807:                     continue
5808: 
5809:                 # A previsão-base mais antiga continua canônica.
5810:                 local_created = str(local["created_at"] or "9999")
5811:                 remote_created = str(remote.get("created_at") or "9999")
5812:                 changes = {}
5813:                 if remote_created < local_created:
5814:                     for k in ("created_at","confidence_score","confidence_label","recommendation","components_json","signals_json","note"):
5815:                         if k in remote:
5816:                             changes[k] = remote.get(k)
5817: 
5818:                 # Para a Decisão Contextual vale o mesmo princípio: a primeira
5819:                 # leitura congelada entre os PCs é a canônica.
5820:                 local_ctx = str(local["contextual_json"] or "").strip()
5821:                 remote_ctx = str(remote.get("contextual_json") or "").strip()
5822:                 local_ctx_at = str(local["contextual_frozen_at"] or "9999")
5823:                 remote_ctx_at = str(remote.get("contextual_frozen_at") or "9999")
5824:                 if remote_ctx and (not local_ctx or remote_ctx_at < local_ctx_at):
5825:                     changes["contextual_json"] = remote.get("contextual_json")
5826:                     changes["contextual_frozen_at"] = remote.get("contextual_frozen_at")
5827: 
5828:                 # GP-H Meta: a primeira leitura congelada entre PCs também é canônica.
5829:                 local_meta = str(local["meta_json"] or "").strip()
5830:                 remote_meta = str(remote.get("meta_json") or "").strip()
5831:                 local_meta_at = str(local["meta_frozen_at"] or "9999")
5832:                 remote_meta_at = str(remote.get("meta_frozen_at") or "9999")
5833:                 if remote_meta and (not local_meta or remote_meta_at < local_meta_at):
5834:                     changes["meta_json"] = remote.get("meta_json")
5835:                     changes["meta_frozen_at"] = remote.get("meta_frozen_at")
5836: 
5837:                 if str(remote.get("status") or "") == "AUDITADO" and str(local["status"] or "") != "AUDITADO":
5838:                     for k in ("status","signals_json","contextual_audit_json","meta_audit_json","result_groups_json","audited_at"):
5839:                         changes[k] = remote.get(k)
5840:                 elif (
5841:                     str(local["status"] or "") == "AUDITADO"
5842:                     and not str(local["contextual_audit_json"] or "").strip()
5843:                     and str(remote.get("contextual_audit_json") or "").strip()
5844:                 ):
5845:                     changes["contextual_audit_json"] = remote.get("contextual_audit_json")
5846:                 if (
5847:                     str(local["status"] or "") == "AUDITADO"
5848:                     and not str(local["meta_audit_json"] or "").strip()
5849:                     and str(remote.get("meta_audit_json") or "").strip()
5850:                 ):
5851:                     changes["meta_audit_json"] = remote.get("meta_audit_json")
5852: 
5853:                 if changes:
5854:                     sets = ",".join(f"{k}=?" for k in changes)
5855:                     con.execute(
5856:                         f"UPDATE decision_snapshots SET {sets} WHERE id=?",
5857:                         list(changes.values()) + [int(local["id"])],
5858:                     )
5859:                     updated += 1
5860:         return added, updated

===== METHOD Database.merge_sync_snapshot [5911:5976] =====
5911:     def merge_sync_snapshot(self, payload):
5912:         """Mescla um snapshot remoto preservando IDs locais e relações."""
5913:         if not isinstance(payload, dict):
5914:             raise ValueError("Pacote de sincronização inválido.")
5915:         if int(payload.get("format_version") or 0) != SYNC_FORMAT_VERSION:
5916:             raise ValueError("Versão de pacote de sincronização incompatível.")
5917: 
5918:         r_add, r_upd = self._merge_sync_results(payload.get("resultados") or [])
5919:         meta_changed = self._merge_sync_meta(payload.get("meta") or [])
5920:         d_add, d_upd = self._merge_sync_decision_snapshots(payload.get("decision_snapshots") or [])
5921:         sh_add, sh_upd = self._merge_sync_shadow_snapshots(payload.get("shadow_snapshots") or [])
5922:         t_add = t_upd = g_add = g_upd = 0
5923:         touched_tickets = set()
5924: 
5925:         with self.connect() as con:
5926:             ticket_by_uid, ticket_by_fp = self._sync_ticket_lookup_maps(con)
5927:             game_by_uid, game_by_fp = self._sync_game_lookup_maps(con)
5928: 
5929:             ticket_local_ids = {}
5930:             for remote in payload.get("bilhetes") or []:
5931:                 local_id, added, updated = self._insert_or_update_sync_ticket(
5932:                     con, remote, ticket_by_uid, ticket_by_fp
5933:                 )
5934:                 uid = str(remote.get("sync_uid") or "")
5935:                 if local_id is not None:
5936:                     ticket_local_ids[uid] = local_id
5937:                     touched_tickets.add(local_id)
5938:                 t_add += int(added)
5939:                 t_upd += int(updated)
5940: 
5941:             # Mapas podem ter mudado após inserções/adopções.
5942:             ticket_by_uid, ticket_by_fp = self._sync_ticket_lookup_maps(con)
5943:             for uid, row in ticket_by_uid.items():
5944:                 ticket_local_ids[uid] = int(row["id"])
5945: 
5946:             for remote in payload.get("jogos") or []:
5947:                 ticket_uid = str(remote.get("bilhete_sync_uid") or "").strip()
5948:                 ticket_id = ticket_local_ids.get(ticket_uid) if ticket_uid else None
5949:                 local_id, added, updated = self._insert_or_update_sync_game(
5950:                     con, remote, ticket_id, game_by_uid, game_by_fp
5951:                 )
5952:                 if ticket_id is not None:
5953:                     touched_tickets.add(ticket_id)
5954:                 g_add += int(added)
5955:                 g_upd += int(updated)
5956: 
5957:         # Totais de Bilhetes são derivados dos jogos e permanecem consistentes.
5958:         for ticket_id in sorted(touched_tickets):
5959:             try:
5960:                 self.refresh_ticket_totals(ticket_id)
5961:             except Exception:
5962:                 pass
5963: 
5964:         return {
5965:             "resultados_adicionados": r_add,
5966:             "resultados_atualizados": r_upd,
5967:             "bilhetes_adicionados": t_add,
5968:             "bilhetes_atualizados": t_upd,
5969:             "jogos_adicionados": g_add,
5970:             "jogos_atualizados": g_upd,
5971:             "configuracoes_atualizadas": meta_changed,
5972:             "decisoes_adicionadas": d_add,
5973:             "decisoes_atualizadas": d_upd,
5974:             "sombras_adicionadas": sh_add,
5975:             "sombras_atualizadas": sh_upd,
5976:         }

===== METHOD Database._financial_snapshot [9244:9334] =====
9244:     def _financial_snapshot(
9245:         self,
9246:         kind,
9247:         scope,
9248:         item_count,
9249:         value_per_item,
9250:         submodalidade=None,
9251:     ):
9252:         stake = float(value_per_item)
9253:         if stake < 0:
9254:             raise ValueError("O valor por palpite não pode ser negativo.")
9255: 
9256:         count = max(0, int(item_count))
9257:         total = stake * count
9258: 
9259:         fixed = kind in FIXED_PLACEMENT_MODALITIES
9260: 
9261:         if fixed:
9262:             divisor = 1
9263:             max_events = count
9264:         else:
9265:             divisor = 5 if scope == "1º–5º" else 1
9266:             max_events = 5 if scope == "1º–5º" else 1
9267: 
9268:         value_position = stake / divisor if divisor else stake
9269: 
9270:         special = (
9271:             kind == "Milhar"
9272:             and submodalidade == "Milhar/Centena"
9273:         )
9274: 
9275:         if special:
9276:             quotes = self.get_milhar_centena_quotes()
9277:             primary = quotes["milhar"]
9278:             secondary = quotes["centena"]
9279:             combined = quotes["ambos"]
9280: 
9281:             min_return = (
9282:                 value_position * secondary
9283:                 if stake > 0 and secondary > 0
9284:                 else None
9285:             )
9286:             max_return = (
9287:                 max_events * value_position * combined
9288:                 if stake > 0 and combined > 0
9289:                 else None
9290:             )
9291: 
9292:         else:
9293:             quote = self.get_quote(kind)
9294:             primary = quote if quote > 0 else None
9295:             secondary = None
9296:             combined = None
9297: 
9298:             one_hit = (
9299:                 value_position * quote
9300:                 if stake > 0 and quote > 0
9301:                 else None
9302:             )
9303: 
9304:             min_return = one_hit
9305: 
9306:             if fixed:
9307:                 max_return = (
9308:                     count * stake * quote
9309:                     if stake > 0 and quote > 0
9310:                     else None
9311:                 )
9312:             else:
9313:                 max_return = (
9314:                     max_events * one_hit
9315:                     if one_hit is not None
9316:                     else None
9317:                 )
9318: 
9319:         return {
9320:             "valor_unitario": stake,
9321:             "valor_total": total,
9322:             "divisor_posicoes": divisor,
9323:             "valor_posicao": value_position,
9324:             "cotacao_primaria": primary,
9325:             "cotacao_secundaria": secondary,
9326:             "cotacao_combinada": combined,
9327:             "multiplicador": primary,
9328:             "retorno_min": min_return,
9329:             "retorno_max": max_return,
9330:             "fixed_placement": fixed,
9331:             "submodalidade": submodalidade,
9332:             "max_eventos": max_events,
9333:             "single_position": (not fixed and max_events == 1),
9334:         }

===== METHOD Database.audit_frozen_games [12005:12066] =====
12005:     def audit_frozen_games(self):
12006:         with self.connect() as con:
12007:             ids = [
12008:                 r["id"]
12009:                 for r in con.execute("""
12010:                     SELECT id
12011:                     FROM jogos_congelados
12012:                     WHERE status='PENDENTE'
12013:                     ORDER BY id
12014:                 """).fetchall()
12015:             ]
12016: 
12017:         audited = []
12018:         pending = []
12019: 
12020:         for game_id in ids:
12021:             result = self.audit_frozen_game(game_id)
12022:             if result["audited"]:
12023:                 audited.append(result)
12024:             else:
12025:                 pending.append(result)
12026: 
12027:         # Auditorias prospectivas auxiliares são silenciosas.
12028:         try:
12029:             shadow_changed = self.audit_shadow_snapshots()
12030:         except Exception:
12031:             shadow_changed = 0
12032:         try:
12033:             decision_changed = self.audit_decision_snapshots()
12034:         except Exception:
12035:             decision_changed = 0
12036: 
12037:         # v0.35.0 — o Laboratório deixa de depender de uma aposta oficial.
12038:         # Sempre que chega/é auditado um resultado, congela silenciosamente a
12039:         # leitura da próxima rodada operacional, ainda sem conhecer o resultado-alvo.
12040:         try:
12041:             _shadow_row, shadow_created = self.ensure_shadow_snapshot(
12042:                 trigger="POS_RESULTADO"
12043:             )
12044:         except Exception:
12045:             shadow_created = False
12046: 
12047:         # v0.40.0 — a inteligência da Decisão também passa a ser coletada
12048:         # automaticamente após cada resultado, sem depender de abrir a tela.
12049:         try:
12050:             _decision_row, decision_created = self.freeze_decision_snapshot(force=False)
12051:             _decision_row, contextual_created = self.freeze_decision_contextual(_decision_row, force=False)
12052:         except Exception:
12053:             decision_created = False
12054:             contextual_created = False
12055: 
12056:         return {
12057:             "audited": audited,
12058:             "pending": pending,
12059:             "audited_count": len(audited),
12060:             "pending_count": len(pending),
12061:             "shadow_audited_count": shadow_changed,
12062:             "shadow_created_count": 1 if shadow_created else 0,
12063:             "decision_audited_count": decision_changed,
12064:             "decision_created_count": 1 if decision_created else 0,
12065:             "contextual_created_count": 1 if contextual_created else 0,
12066:         }

===== METHOD Database.audit [12069:12136] =====
12069:     def audit(self):
12070:         problems = []
12071:         with self.connect() as con:
12072:             total = con.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM resultados").fetchone()[0]
12073:             min_d, max_d = con.execute("SELECT MIN(data), MAX(data) FROM resultados").fetchone()
12074:             draws = con.execute("""
12075:                 SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
12076:                     SELECT DISTINCT data,sorteio,hora FROM resultados
12077:                 )
12078:             """).fetchone()[0]
12079: 
12080:             dup = con.execute("""
12081:                 SELECT COUNT(*) FROM (
12082:                     SELECT data,sorteio,hora,premio,COUNT(*) c
12083:                     FROM resultados
12084:                     GROUP BY data,sorteio,hora,premio
12085:                     HAVING c > 1
12086:                 )
12087:             """).fetchone()[0]
12088:             if dup:
12089:                 problems.append(f"{dup} chave(s) duplicada(s).")
12090: 
12091:             invalid = con.execute("""
12092:                 SELECT id, milhar, centena, dezena, grupo, bicho
12093:                 FROM resultados
12094:                 WHERE LENGTH(milhar)<>4 OR LENGTH(centena)<>3 OR LENGTH(dezena)<>2
12095:                    OR grupo<1 OR grupo>25
12096:             """).fetchall()
12097:             if invalid:
12098:                 problems.append(f"{len(invalid)} registro(s) com formato inválido.")
12099: 
12100:             mismatch = 0
12101:             rows = con.execute("SELECT milhar, grupo, bicho FROM resultados").fetchall()
12102:             for r in rows:
12103:                 try:
12104:                     _, _, _, g, b = derivados_milhar(r["milhar"])
12105:                     if g != r["grupo"] or sem_acento(b) != sem_acento(r["bicho"]):
12106:                         mismatch += 1
12107:                 except Exception:
12108:                     mismatch += 1
12109:             if mismatch:
12110:                 problems.append(f"{mismatch} registro(s) com grupo/bicho incompatível com a dezena.")
12111: 
12112:             pub_mismatch = con.execute("""
12113:                 SELECT COUNT(*) FROM resultados
12114:                 WHERE grupo_publicado IS NOT NULL AND grupo_publicado <> grupo
12115:             """).fetchone()[0]
12116:             if pub_mismatch:
12117:                 problems.append(
12118:                     f"{pub_mismatch} divergência(s) entre o grupo publicado e o grupo calculado."
12119:                 )
12120: 
12121:             incomplete = con.execute("""
12122:                 SELECT data,sorteio,hora,COUNT(*) c
12123:                 FROM resultados
12124:                 GROUP BY data,sorteio,hora
12125:                 HAVING c <> 5
12126:                 ORDER BY data,hora
12127:             """).fetchall()
12128: 
12129:         return {
12130:             "total": total,
12131:             "draws": draws,
12132:             "min_date": min_d,
12133:             "max_date": max_d,
12134:             "problems": problems,
12135:             "incomplete": [tuple(r) for r in incomplete],
12136:         }

===== METHOD App._shadow_freeze_async [17645:17657] =====
17645:     def _shadow_freeze_async(self, target, base_draw):
17646:         """Congela o Laboratório Sombra sem atrasar a geração visível do usuário."""
17647:         if not target or not base_draw:
17648:             return
17649:         target_copy = copy.deepcopy(target)
17650:         base_copy = {k: base_draw.get(k) for k in ("data","sorteio","hora")}
17651:         def worker():
17652:             try:
17653:                 self.db.ensure_shadow_snapshot(target_copy, base_copy, trigger="GERAR_JOGO")
17654:             except Exception:
17655:                 # Laboratório é diagnóstico; nunca pode impedir uma aposta real.
17656:                 pass
17657:         threading.Thread(target=worker, daemon=True).start()

===== METHOD App._shadow_recommendation_current_context [17659:17730] =====
17659:     def _shadow_recommendation_current_context(self, target, scope, best_name):
17660:         # Explica a recomendação com sinais atuais sem mudar o vencedor histórico.
17661:         try:
17662:             status = self.db.shadow_lab_status(target)
17663:             current = (status or {}).get("current") or {}
17664:             payload = current.get("payload") or {}
17665:             bpayload = payload.get("bichos") or {}
17666: 
17667:             method_groups = {}
17668:             support = Counter()
17669:             for method in ("Reset Cobertura", "Puxada Combinada", "Similaridade"):
17670:                 rec = bpayload.get(method) or {}
17671:                 groups = []
17672:                 for value in rec.get("groups") or []:
17673:                     try:
17674:                         group = int(value)
17675:                     except Exception:
17676:                         continue
17677:                     if 1 <= group <= 25 and group not in groups:
17678:                         groups.append(group)
17679:                 groups = groups[:5]
17680:                 if groups:
17681:                     method_groups[method] = groups
17682:                     support.update(groups)
17683: 
17684:             parts = []
17685:             available_count = len(method_groups)
17686:             convergent = sorted(
17687:                 ((group, count) for group, count in support.items() if count >= 2),
17688:                 key=lambda item: (-item[1], item[0]),
17689:             )
17690:             if available_count >= 2:
17691:                 if convergent:
17692:                     labels = [
17693:                         f"{BICHOS.get(group, str(group)).title()} {count}/{available_count}"
17694:                         for group, count in convergent[:5]
17695:                     ]
17696:                     parts.append("Convergência atual: " + ", ".join(labels))
17697:                 else:
17698:                     parts.append(f"Convergência atual: nenhuma coincidência entre {available_count} métodos")
17699: 
17700:             method_map = {
17701:                 "Reset + Histórica": "Reset Cobertura",
17702:                 "Puxada + Histórica": "Puxada Combinada",
17703:                 "Similaridade + Histórica": "Similaridade",
17704:             }
17705:             chosen_method = method_map.get(best_name, best_name if best_name in method_groups else None)
17706:             if chosen_method in method_groups and available_count >= 2:
17707:                 groups = method_groups[chosen_method]
17708:                 shared = [group for group in groups if support.get(group, 0) >= 2]
17709:                 parts.append(f"Apoio ao recomendado: {len(shared)}/{len(groups)} bichos reforçados por outro método")
17710: 
17711:             if str(scope or "") == "1º":
17712:                 dry = ((payload.get("seca_1p") or {}).get("Seca do Dia 1º") or {})
17713:                 dry_groups = []
17714:                 for value in dry.get("groups") or []:
17715:                     try:
17716:                         group = int(value)
17717:                     except Exception:
17718:                         continue
17719:                     if 1 <= group <= 25 and group not in dry_groups:
17720:                         dry_groups.append(group)
17721:                 dry_groups = dry_groups[:5]
17722:                 if dry_groups:
17723:                     dry_names = ", ".join(BICHOS.get(group, str(group)).title() for group in dry_groups)
17724:                     overlap = [group for group in dry_groups if support.get(group, 0) >= 2]
17725:                     suffix = f" • {len(overlap)} também na convergência" if convergent else ""
17726:                     parts.append(f"Seca 1º atual: {dry_names}{suffix}")
17727: 
17728:             return " • ".join(parts)
17729:         except Exception:
17730:             return ""

17784:     def _play_refresh_shadow_recommendation(self):
17785:         # A recomendacao principal vem da Etapa D. O ranking sombra abaixo

17805:             candidates = []

17838: 
17839:             # A Seca só concorre como indicação principal quando o bilhete está no 1º prêmio.
17840:             dry = rec.get("seca_1p") or {}
17841:             if scope == "1º" and int(dry.get("rounds") or 0):
17842:                 status = dry.get("status", "SEM DADOS")
17843:                 candidates.append({
17844:                     "name": "Seca do Dia 1º",
17845:                     "status": status,
17846:                     "rounds": int(dry.get("rounds") or 0),
17847:                     "score": rank.get(status, 0),
17848:                     "detail": f"{float(dry.get('hit_rate') or 0):.1f}% de acerto do 1º prêmio no recorte prospectivo",
17849:                 })
17850: 
17851:             if not candidates:
17852:                 label.configure(text="Ainda não há amostra suficiente para recomendar um método nesta rodada.")
17853:                 if note is not None:
17854:                     note.configure(text="A Central continua coletando jogos-sombra antes dos resultados.")
17855:                 return
17856: 
17857:             # Primeiro qualidade da evidência; depois tamanho da amostra.
17858:             candidates.sort(key=lambda c: (-c["score"], -c["rounds"], c["name"]))
17859:             best = candidates[0]
17860:             if best["score"] <= 1:
17861:                 label.configure(text="Ainda não há um método claramente superior para esta rodada.")
17862:                 if note is not None:
17863:                     note.configure(text=f"Melhor sinal provisório: {best['name']} • {best['rounds']} rodadas. Amostra insuficiente.")
17864:                 return
17865: 
17866:             label.configure(
17867:                 text=f"Melhor indicação agora: {best['name']} • evidência {best['status']}"
17868:             )
17869:             if note is not None:
17870:                 base_note = f"{best['rounds']} rodadas prospectivas neste horário • {best['detail']}."
17871:                 current_context = self._shadow_recommendation_current_context(target, scope, best.get("name"))
17872:                 if current_context:
17873:                     base_note += f" • {current_context}."
17874:                 base_note += " Evidência histórica, não garantia de acerto."
17875:                 note.configure(text=base_note)
17876:         except Exception:
17877:             label.configure(text="Ainda não há amostra suficiente para recomendar um método nesta rodada.")
17878:             if note is not None:
17879:                 note.configure(text="A recomendação aparece somente quando existe evidência prospectiva anterior ao resultado.")

===== METHOD App._auto_decision_cycle [23473:23485] =====
23473:     def _auto_decision_cycle(self):
23474:         try:
23475:             if self.db.count() == 0:
23476:                 return
23477:             self.db.audit_decision_snapshots()
23478:             target = self.db.next_operational_target()
23479:             if target and not self.db.get_draw(target["data"], target["sorteio"], target["hora"]):
23480:                 snapshot, _created = self.db.freeze_decision_snapshot(force=False)
23481:                 snapshot, _ctx = self.db.freeze_decision_contextual(snapshot, force=False)
23482:                 self.db.freeze_meta_snapshot(snapshot, force=False)
23483:         except Exception:
23484:             # Falta de amostra não deve interromper a abertura da Central.
23485:             return

===== METHOD App._decision_target_text [23488:23495] =====
23488:     def _decision_target_text(snapshot):
23489:         if not snapshot:
23490:             return "—"
23491:         try:
23492:             d = datetime.strptime(snapshot["target_data"], "%Y-%m-%d").strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
23493:         except Exception:
23494:             d = snapshot.get("target_data") or "—"
23495:         return f"{snapshot.get('target_sorteio','—')} {snapshot.get('target_hora','—')} • {d}"

===== METHOD App._shadow_lab_target_label [23498:23505] =====

23499:         if not target:
23500:             return "—"
23501:         try:

23503:         except Exception:
23504:             d = str(target.get("data") or "—")
23505:         return f"{d} • {target.get('sorteio','—')} {target.get('hora','—')}"

24211:             return self._page=="decision" and self.meta_wf_tree.winfo_exists()
24212:         except Exception:
24213:             return False

===== METHOD App._meta_walk_forward_start [24215:24255] =====
24215:     def _meta_walk_forward_start(self):
24216:         if getattr(self,"_meta_wf_running",False):
24217:             return
24218:         try:
24219:             window=int(self.meta_wf_window.get())
24220:         except Exception:
24221:             window=30
24222: 
24223:         self._meta_wf_running=True
24224:         self._meta_wf_progress_state=(0,1,"Preparando o histórico do Meta…")
24225:         self._meta_wf_dead_polls=0
24226:         if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24227:             self.meta_wf_run_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24228:             self.meta_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="normal")
24229:             self.meta_wf_export_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24230:             self.meta_wf_progress.configure(value=0)
24231:             self.meta_wf_status.configure(text="Iniciando processo isolado do Walk-Forward…")
24232: 
24233:         try:
24234:             # v0.42.1: CPU-bound Meta sai do processo do Tk. Em thread, o treino
24235:             # Python puro disputava o GIL com a interface e causava microtravadas.
24236:             ctx=mp.get_context("spawn")
24237:             self._meta_wf_queue=ctx.Queue()
24238:             self._meta_wf_cancel_event=ctx.Event()
24239:             proc=ctx.Process(
24240:                 target=_meta_walk_forward_process_worker,
24241:                 args=(str(self.db.path),window,self._meta_wf_queue,self._meta_wf_cancel_event),
24242:                 daemon=True,
24243:                 name="GPH-MetaWalkForward",
24244:             )
24245:             self._meta_wf_process=proc
24246:             proc.start()
24247:         except Exception as exc:
24248:             self._meta_wf_running=False
24249:             self._meta_wf_process=None
24250:             if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24251:                 self.meta_wf_status.configure(text="Falha ao iniciar o processo do Walk-Forward: "+str(exc))
24252:                 self.meta_wf_run_btn.configure(state="normal")
24253:                 self.meta_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24254:             return
24255:         self.after(120,self._meta_walk_forward_poll)

===== METHOD App._meta_walk_forward_cancel [24257:24265] =====
24257:     def _meta_walk_forward_cancel(self):
24258:         event=getattr(self,"_meta_wf_cancel_event",None)
24259:         if event is not None:
24260:             try:
24261:                 event.set()
24262:             except Exception:
24263:                 pass
24264:         if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24265:             self.meta_wf_status.configure(text="Cancelamento solicitado…")

===== METHOD App._meta_walk_forward_process_cleanup [24267:24282] =====
24267:     def _meta_walk_forward_process_cleanup(self):
24268:         proc=getattr(self,"_meta_wf_process",None)
24269:         if proc is not None:
24270:             try:
24271:                 proc.join(timeout=0.15)
24272:             except Exception:
24273:                 pass
24274:         self._meta_wf_process=None
24275:         q=getattr(self,"_meta_wf_queue",None)
24276:         if q is not None:
24277:             try:
24278:                 q.close()
24279:             except Exception:
24280:                 pass
24281:         self._meta_wf_queue=None
24282:         self._meta_wf_cancel_event=None

===== METHOD App._meta_walk_forward_poll [24284:24340] =====
24284:     def _meta_walk_forward_poll(self):
24285:         q=getattr(self,"_meta_wf_queue",None)
24286:         if q is None:
24287:             return
24288:         finished=False
24289:         while True:
24290:             try:
24291:                 msg=q.get_nowait()
24292:             except queue.Empty:
24293:                 break
24294:             except (EOFError,OSError):
24295:                 break
24296:             kind=msg[0]
24297:             if kind=="progress":
24298:                 _k,done,total,label=msg
24299:                 self._meta_wf_progress_state=(done,total,label)
24300:                 if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24301:                     self.meta_wf_progress.configure(value=(done/max(1,total))*100.0)
24302:                     self.meta_wf_status.configure(text=label)
24303:             elif kind=="done":
24304:                 self.meta_walk_forward_last_result=msg[1]
24305:                 self._meta_wf_running=False
24306:                 finished=True
24307:                 if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24308:                     self._meta_walk_forward_render(msg[1])
24309:             elif kind=="error":
24310:                 self._meta_wf_running=False
24311:                 finished=True
24312:                 if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24313:                     self.meta_wf_status.configure(text="Falha no Walk-Forward Meta: "+str(msg[1]))
24314:                     self.meta_wf_run_btn.configure(state="normal")
24315:                     self.meta_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24316: 
24317:         proc=getattr(self,"_meta_wf_process",None)
24318:         if finished:
24319:             self._meta_walk_forward_process_cleanup()
24320:             return
24321: 
24322:         # Se o worker caiu sem conseguir enviar a mensagem de erro, não deixa a
24323:         # interface presa para sempre em "processando". Exit 0 recebe alguns polls
24324:         # extras para a fila terminar de descarregar o resultado.
24325:         if proc is not None and not proc.is_alive():
24326:             exitcode=proc.exitcode
24327:             self._meta_wf_dead_polls=int(getattr(self,"_meta_wf_dead_polls",0))+1
24328:             if exitcode not in (None,0) or self._meta_wf_dead_polls>=5:
24329:                 self._meta_wf_running=False
24330:                 if self._meta_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24331:                     self.meta_wf_status.configure(text=f"O processo do Walk-Forward encerrou inesperadamente (código {exitcode}).")
24332:                     self.meta_wf_run_btn.configure(state="normal")
24333:                     self.meta_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24334:                 self._meta_walk_forward_process_cleanup()
24335:                 return
24336:         else:
24337:             self._meta_wf_dead_polls=0
24338: 
24339:         if getattr(self,"_meta_wf_running",False):
24340:             self.after(120,self._meta_walk_forward_poll)

===== METHOD App._meta_walk_forward_render [24342:24377] =====

24368:         else:

24370:         self.meta_wf_progress.configure(value=100 if not result.get("cancelled") else self.meta_wf_progress["value"])
24371:         self.meta_wf_status.configure(text=status)

24374:             "O cérebro META_LOGIT_NATIVE_V2_TABLE1 é o mesmo da v0.41; este simulador só mede desempenho e não alimenta nem altera o Meta prospectivo."
24375:         ))

===== METHOD App._decision_walk_forward_start [24973:25003] =====
24973:     def _decision_walk_forward_start(self):
24974:         if getattr(self,"_walk_forward_running",False):
24975:             return
24976:         try:
24977:             window=int(self.decision_wf_window.get())
24978:         except Exception:
24979:             window=120
24980:         self._walk_forward_running=True
24981:         self._walk_forward_cancel_event=threading.Event()
24982:         self._walk_forward_queue=queue.Queue()
24983:         self._walk_forward_progress_state=(0,max(1,window),"Preparando o walk-forward…")
24984:         if self._decision_walk_forward_ui_alive():
24985:             self.decision_wf_run_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24986:             self.decision_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="normal")
24987:             self.decision_wf_export_btn.configure(state="disabled")
24988:             self.decision_wf_progress.configure(value=0)
24989:             self.decision_wf_status.configure(text="Preparando as rodadas históricas…")
24990: 
24991:         q=self._walk_forward_queue
24992:         cancel_event=self._walk_forward_cancel_event
24993:         def progress(done,total,base):
24994:             label=f"{done}/{total} • {base.get('data','—')} {base.get('sorteio','—')} {base.get('hora','—')}"
24995:             q.put(("progress",done,total,label))
24996:         def worker():
24997:             try:
24998:                 result=self.db.decision_walk_forward(window=window,progress_callback=progress,cancel_event=cancel_event)
24999:                 q.put(("done",result))
25000:             except Exception as exc:
25001:                 q.put(("error",str(exc)))
25002:         threading.Thread(target=worker,daemon=True,name="GPH-WalkForward").start()
25003:         self.after(100,self._decision_walk_forward_poll)

===== METHOD App._decision_walk_forward_cancel [25005:25011] =====
25005:     def _decision_walk_forward_cancel(self):
25006:         event=getattr(self,"_walk_forward_cancel_event",None)
25007:         if event is not None:
25008:             event.set()
25009:             if self._decision_walk_forward_ui_alive():
25010:                 self.decision_wf_status.configure(text="Cancelando após a rodada atual…")
25011:                 self.decision_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="disabled")

===== METHOD App._decision_walk_forward_poll [25013:25046] =====
25013:     def _decision_walk_forward_poll(self):
25014:         q=getattr(self,"_walk_forward_queue",None)
25015:         if q is None:
25016:             return
25017:         terminal=False
25018:         while True:
25019:             try:
25020:                 msg=q.get_nowait()
25021:             except queue.Empty:
25022:                 break
25023:             kind=msg[0]
25024:             if kind=="progress":
25025:                 _,done,total,label=msg
25026:                 self._walk_forward_progress_state=(done,total,label)
25027:                 if self._decision_walk_forward_ui_alive():
25028:                     self.decision_wf_progress.configure(value=(done/max(1,total))*100.0)
25029:                     self.decision_wf_status.configure(text=label)
25030:             elif kind=="done":
25031:                 terminal=True
25032:                 self._walk_forward_running=False
25033:                 self.walk_forward_last_result=msg[1]
25034:                 if self._decision_walk_forward_ui_alive():
25035:                     self.decision_wf_run_btn.configure(state="normal")
25036:                     self.decision_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="disabled")
25037:                     self._decision_walk_forward_render(msg[1])
25038:             elif kind=="error":
25039:                 terminal=True
25040:                 self._walk_forward_running=False
25041:                 if self._decision_walk_forward_ui_alive():
25042:                     self.decision_wf_run_btn.configure(state="normal")
25043:                     self.decision_wf_cancel_btn.configure(state="disabled")
25044:                     self.decision_wf_status.configure(text=f"Falha na simulação: {msg[1]}")
25045:         if getattr(self,"_walk_forward_running",False) and not terminal:
25046:             self.after(120,self._decision_walk_forward_poll)

===== METHOD App._decision_walk_forward_render [25048:25093] =====
25048:     def _decision_walk_forward_render(self,result):
25049:         if not self._decision_walk_forward_ui_alive():
25050:             return
25051:         tree=self.decision_wf_tree
25052:         for item in tree.get_children():
25053:             tree.delete(item)
25054:         for r in result.get("summary") or []:
25055:             pos="—" if r.get("avg_position") is None else f"{r['avg_position']:.2f}/5"
25056:             uplift=float(r.get("uplift_vs_random") or 0.0)
25057:             tree.insert("","end",values=(
25058:                 r.get("method"),r.get("rounds",0),f"{r.get('avg_coverage',0):.2f}/5",
25059:                 f"{r.get('pct_2plus',0):.0f}%",f"{r.get('pct_3plus',0):.0f}%",
25060:                 f"{r.get('pct_4plus',0):.0f}%",f"{r.get('pct_5',0):.0f}%",
25061:                 f"{uplift:+.2f}",pos,
25062:             ))
25063:         robust=getattr(self,"decision_wf_robust_tree",None)
25064:         if robust is not None:
25065:             for item in robust.get_children():
25066:                 robust.delete(item)
25067:             for r in result.get("robustness") or []:
25068:                 robust.insert("","end",values=(
25069:                     r.get("method"),f"{r.get('first_avg',0):.2f}/5 ({r.get('first_rounds',0)})",
25070:                     f"{r.get('second_avg',0):.2f}/5 ({r.get('second_rounds',0)})",
25071:                     f"{r.get('delta',0):+.2f}",f"{(r.get('second_p2',0)-r.get('first_p2',0)):+.0f} pp",
25072:                 ))
25073:         simulated=int(result.get("simulated_rounds") or 0)
25074:         requested=int(result.get("requested_rounds") or 0)
25075:         paired=int(result.get("paired_rounds") or 0)
25076:         best=result.get("best_paired") or {}
25077:         if result.get("cancelled"):
25078:             status=f"Simulação cancelada • {simulated}/{requested} rodada(s) processada(s)."
25079:         else:
25080:             status=f"Concluído • {simulated} rodada(s) processada(s) • {paired} rodada(s) comparáveis entre os três métodos."
25081:         self.decision_wf_status.configure(text=status)
25082:         self.decision_wf_progress.configure(value=100 if simulated else 0)
25083:         if best:
25084:             note=(
25085:                 f"Melhor no recorte pareado: {best.get('method')} ({best.get('avg_coverage',0):.2f}/5). "
25086:                 f"Período dos alvos: {result.get('date_from') or '—'} a {result.get('date_to') or '—'}. "
25087:                 "'vs. acaso' é a diferença para a sobreposição esperada de uma seleção aleatória com o mesmo nº de grupos únicos. "
25088:                 "Resultado histórico não garante desempenho futuro."
25089:             )
25090:         else:
25091:             note="Ainda não houve rodadas comparáveis suficientes."
25092:         self.decision_wf_note.configure(text=note)
25093:         self.decision_wf_export_btn.configure(state="normal" if simulated else "disabled")

25121:             for r in result.get("robustness") or []:

26757:     def show_audit(self, report=None):
26758:         audit = report["audit"] if report else self.db.audit()

26773:             f"Extrações: {audit['draws']:,}\n"
26774:         ).replace(",", ".")
26775:

26777:             text += "\nProblemas encontrados:\n- " + "\n- ".join(problems)
26778:         else:
26779:             text += "\n✓ Nenhuma inconsistência estrutural encontrada."
26780: 
26781:         if inc:
26782:             # Uma extração do dia atual pode estar incompleta enquanto o site atualiza.
26783:             text += f"\n\nAtenção: {len(inc)} extração(ões) com quantidade diferente de 5 prêmios."
26784:             for d, s, h, c in inc[:8]:
26785:                 text += f"\n- {d} • {s} {h}: {c} prêmio(s)"
26786:             if len(inc) > 8:
26787:                 text += f"\n- ... e mais {len(inc)-8}."
26788: 
26789:         if report:
26790:             text += (
26791:                 f"\n\nSincronização:\n"
26792:                 f"Dias baixados/relidos: {report['fetched_days']}\n"
26793:                 f"Dias antigos já existentes e pulados: {report['skipped_days']}\n"
26794:                 f"Linhas processadas: {report['processed_rows']}\n"
26795:                 f"Falhas de acesso à página: {len(report['errors'])}"
26796:             )
26797:             if report["errors"]:
26798:                 text += (
26799:                     "\n\nEssas falhas indicam dias que não puderam ser "
26800:                     "verificados nessa tentativa; não significam, sozinhas, "
26801:                     "que o resultado esteja ausente da base."
26802:                 )
26803:                 text += "\n\nPrimeiras falhas:\n- " + "\n- ".join(report["errors"][:8])
26804: 
26805:         self.status.configure(

218: def _sha256_file(path):
219:     h = hashlib.sha256()
220:     with Path(path).open("rb") as fh:
